### [1] [Task-Oriented Wireless Transmission of 3D Point Clouds: Geometric Versus Semantic Robustness](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.13560)
*Vukan Ninkovic,Tamara Sobot,Vladimir Vincan,Gorana Gojic,Dragisa Miskovic,Dejan Vukobratovic*

Main category: eess.SP

TL;DR: 该论文提出了一种用于无线3D点云传输的端到端语义通信框架，研究发现语义推理在几何重建质量显著下降时仍能保持稳定，表明可靠的任务执行不需要高保真几何恢复。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 工业协作机器人系统需要无线传输高维3D点云，传统压缩方法优先考虑几何保真度，但许多实际应用最终依赖于可靠的任务级推理而非精确坐标重建。需要研究几何重建保真度与语义鲁棒性之间的关系。

Method: 提出端到端语义通信框架，支持从共享传输表示中联合进行几何恢复和对象分类，能够直接比较坐标级和任务级对噪声的敏感性。

Result: 在真实工业数据集上的实验评估显示明显的不对称性：即使在几何重建质量显著下降时，语义推理在广泛的信噪比范围内仍保持稳定。

Conclusion: 可靠的任务执行不需要高保真几何恢复，这为带宽和功率受限的工业环境中的面向任务的无线感知系统提供了设计见解。

Abstract: Wireless transmission of high-dimensional 3D point clouds (PCs) is increasingly required in industrial collaborative robotics systems. Conventional compression methods prioritize geometric fidelity, although many practical applications ultimately depend on reliable task-level inference rather than exact coordinate reconstruction. In this paper, we propose an end-to-end semantic communication framework for wireless 3D PC transmission and conduct a systematic study of the relationship between geometric reconstruction fidelity and semantic robustness under channel impairments. The proposed architecture jointly supports geometric recovery and object classification from a shared transmitted representation, enabling direct comparison between coordinate-level and task-level sensitivity to noise. Experimental evaluation on a real industrial dataset reveals a pronounced asymmetry: semantic inference remains stable across a broad signal-to-noise ratio (SNR) range even when geometric reconstruction quality degrades significantly. These results demonstrate that reliable task execution does not require high-fidelity geometric recovery and provide design insights for task-oriented wireless perception systems in bandwidth- and power-constrained industrial environments.

</details>


### [2] [Extended Target Sensing in MIMO-OFDM ISAC Systems: Modeling, Optimization and Estimation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.13580)
*Rang Liu,Ming Li,A. Lee Swindlehurst*

Main category: eess.SP

TL;DR: 本文针对宽带MIMO-OFDM通感一体化系统中的扩展目标感知，提出了参数化散射模型、混合贝叶斯CRB推导、距离模糊抑制约束和模糊感知波束成形设计，显著提升了目标定位性能并降低了计算复杂度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 宽带MIMO-OFDM通感一体化系统中扩展目标感知面临挑战：传统模型复杂度高，OFDM信号存在距离模糊问题，需要同时满足通信SINR要求和感知性能优化。

Method: 1) 提出参数化散射模型，将目标几何与电磁散射特性解耦；2) 推导混合贝叶斯CRB用于联合参数估计；3) 分析距离模糊函数并引入距离旁瓣抑制约束；4) 设计模糊感知发射波束成形，最小化加权几何CRB；5) 扩展两种基准模型并进行比较。

Result: 仿真结果表明：所提PSM方法在目标定位性能上优于基准方法，波束成形优化和参数估计的运行时间显著减少，同时始终满足通信SINR要求。

Conclusion: 提出的框架为宽带MIMO-OFDM通感一体化系统提供了有效的扩展目标感知解决方案，通过参数化建模、模糊抑制和联合优化设计，实现了感知性能与计算效率的良好平衡。

Abstract: This paper develops a comprehensive target modeling, beamforming optimization, and parameter estimation framework for extended-target sensing in wideband MIMO-OFDM integrated sensing and communication systems. We propose a parametric scattering model (PSM) that decouples target geometry from electromagnetic scattering characteristics, requiring only six nonlinear geometric parameters and linear radar cross-section (RCS) coefficients. Based on this compact structure, we derive a hybrid Bayesian Cramér-Rao bound (CRB) for joint estimation of azimuth, elevation, and range-related parameters. To handle inherent range ambiguities due to OFDM signaling, we analyze the range ambiguity function and introduce range sidelobe suppression constraints around the true range. Based on these constraints, we formulate an ambiguity-aware transmit beamforming design that minimizes a weighted geometric CRB subject to per-user signal-to-interference-plus-noise ratio (SINR) requirements and a total power budget. As benchmarks, we extend two other common models to the same wideband MIMO-OFDM scenario. We also derive maximum a posteriori estimators and a computational complexity analysis for all three models. Simulation results demonstrate that the proposed PSM-based approach achieves improved target localization with significantly reduced runtime for beamforming optimization and parameter estimation, while consistently satisfying communication SINR requirements.

</details>


### [3] [Expressivity of Programmable-Metasurface-Based Physical Neural Networks: Encoding Non-Linearity, Structural Non-Linearity, and Depth](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.13602)
*Cheima Hammami,Luc Le Magoarou,Christos Monochristou,David González-Ovejero,Ali Momeni,Romain Fleury,Philipp del Hougne*

Main category: eess.SP

TL;DR: 基于波的物理神经网络（WPNN）通过结构输入编码实现非线性映射，研究编码非线性、结构非线性和网络深度的相互作用对表达力的影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统波信号处理将输入数据编码到输入波前，难以实现非线性操作。结构输入编码将数据编码到可调组件的散射特性中，涉及两个潜在的非线性映射，需要系统研究这种WPNN的表达能力。

Method: 基于物理一致的多端口网络模型，使用100单元可编程超表面参数化的D波段丰富散射腔。分别控制编码非线性、结构非线性和网络深度，考虑受控标量回归任务，分析它们的相互作用。

Result: 相位编码和强元件间互耦时，两个映射都高度非线性，单层WPNN表现优异。额外层可以部分补偿弱互耦。网络深度即使不增加可训练权重也能提高表达能力。

Conclusion: 编码选择、互耦强度和深度共同决定基于可编程超表面的WPNN的表达能力，为未来实验实现提供设计指导。

Abstract: Wave-based signal processing conventionally encodes input data into the input wavefront, making it challenging to implement non-linear operations. Programmable wave systems enable an alternative approach: encoding the input data into the scattering properties of tunable components. With such structural input encoding, two potentially non-linear mappings are involved: first, from the input data to the tunable components' scattering characteristics, and, second, from these scattering characteristics to the output wavefront. In this paper, we systematically examine the expressivity of a wave-based physical neural network (WPNN) with structural input encoding. Our analysis is based on a physics-consistent multiport-network model of a compact D-band rich-scattering cavity parametrized by a 100-element programmable metasurface. We separately control encoding non-linearity, structural non-linearity, and network depth in order to examine their interplay, considering a controlled scalar regression task. With phase encoding and strong inter-element mutual coupling (MC), both aforementioned mappings are strongly non-linear and the WPNN performs very well even with a single layer. We further observe that additional layers can partially compensate for weak inter-element MC. In addition, we demonstrate that WPNN depth can improve expressivity even when it is not associated with an increase in trainable weights. Altogether, our results provide a physics-consistent picture of how encoding choice, MC strength, and depth jointly govern the expressive power of PM-based WPNNs, informing design choices for future experimental implementations of WPNNs.

</details>


### [4] [Online Model Predictive Control for Trajectory and Beamforming Optimization in UAV-Enabled URLLC](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.13731)
*Asim Ihsan,Muhammad Asif,Ali Arshad Nasir,Khaled M. Rabie,Wali Ullah Khan*

Main category: eess.SP

TL;DR: 该论文研究了在有限块长度传输下，无人机辅助超可靠低延迟通信系统的联合轨迹和主动波束成形设计，提出了基于模型预测控制的闭环优化框架。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统香农容量公式在有限块长度传输下不适用，因为FBL机制引入了依赖于SINR的色散惩罚，增加了可靠性对移动性引起的信道变化的敏感性。需要开发能够处理FBL约束的无人机轨迹和波束成形联合优化方法。

Method: 提出了推进感知的模型预测控制框架，采用滚动时域优化方法。将非凸问题分解为波束成形和轨迹子问题，通过交替优化求解。为香农容量项构建凹代理函数，为色散项和非线性推进功率模型推导凸近似，得到可处理的凸子问题迭代求解。

Result: 与离线MPC基线和传统离线轨迹-波束成形优化相比，所提出的闭环框架在无人机位置扰动下实现了抗干扰的任务完成。仿真结果表明，与最大比传输和等功率分配相比，所提出的干扰感知设计在严格的最小速率约束下显著提高了URLLC可靠性。

Conclusion: 该研究为5G及以后的可靠性中心无人机无线网络提供了设计见解，量化了天线缩放、发射功率和传输时间对FBL性能的影响，展示了闭环MPC框架在实现抗干扰URLLC通信方面的有效性。

Abstract: This paper investigates joint trajectory and active beamforming design for unmanned aerial vehicle (UAV)-enabled ultra-reliable low-latency communication (URLLC) systems under finite blocklength (FBL) transmission. Unlike conventional Shannon-capacity formulations, the FBL regime introduces a signal-to-interference-plus-noise ratio (SINR)-dependent dispersion penalty that increases the sensitivity of reliability to mobility-induced channel variations. To address this challenge, we develop a propulsion-aware model predictive control (MPC) framework that performs receding-horizon joint trajectory and multi-user beamforming optimization while enforcing FBL-based rate constraints. The resulting long-horizon nonconvex problem is decomposed into beamforming and trajectory subproblems using alternating optimization. Concave surrogate is constructed for the Shannon-capacity term, while convex approximations are derived for the dispersion term and the nonlinear propulsion power model, yielding tractable convex subproblems solved iteratively. Compared with an offline MPC baseline, where the predictive problem is solved once over the entire mission horizon without feedback updates, and a conventional offline trajectory-beamforming optimization, the proposed closed-loop framework achieves disturbance-resilient mission completion under UAV position disturbances. Simulation results show that, compared with maximum ratio transmission (MRT) and equal-power allocation, the proposed interference-aware design significantly improves URLLC reliability under stringent minimum rate constraints. The results also quantify the impact of antenna scaling, transmit power, and transmission time on FBL performance, providing insights for reliability-centric UAV-enabled wireless networks in 5G and beyond.

</details>


### [5] [Active Beyond-Diagonal Reconfigurable Intelligent Surfaces: Modeling, Architecture Design, and Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.13861)
*Shanpu Shen,Hongyu Li,Matteo Nerini,Qingqing Wu,Bruno Clerckx*

Main category: eess.SP

TL;DR: 本文研究了有源超对角可重构智能表面（BD-RIS）技术，通过引入有源放大功能解决传统无源RIS面临的乘性路径损耗问题，提出了两种新架构并开发了优化算法，显著提升了频谱效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统BD-RIS主要采用无源设计，缺乏主动放大功能，导致严重的乘性路径损耗问题。为了解决这一关键挑战，本文研究有源BD-RIS技术，旨在通过引入有源元件来增强信号传输性能。

Method: 1. 使用多端口网络理论和散射参数分析有源BD-RIS，推导出物理和电磁兼容的通信模型；2. 设计了两种新架构：全连接和分组连接有源BD-RIS；3. 针对SISO系统推导闭式最优解和信噪比缩放规律；4. 针对MIMO系统提出基于二次约束二次规划的迭代算法来最大化频谱效率。

Result: 数值结果表明，有源BD-RIS在频谱效率上优于有源/无源对角RIS和无源BD-RIS。为实现相同频谱效率，有源BD-RIS所需元件数量仅为有源对角RIS的一半以下，显示出显著优势。

Conclusion: 有源BD-RIS通过引入有源放大功能有效解决了传统RIS的乘性路径损耗问题，提出的新架构和优化算法显著提升了无线通信系统的性能，为未来RIS 2.0技术的发展提供了重要方向。

Abstract: Beyond-diagonal reconfigurable intelligent surfaces (BD-RISs) are an emerging RIS 2.0 technology for future wireless communication. However, BD-RISs are primarily passive without active amplification, suffering from severe multiplicative path loss. To address the concern of multiplicative path loss, in this work we investigate the active BD-RIS including the modeling, architecture design, and optimization. We first analyze the active BD-RIS using multiport network theory with scattering parameters and derive a physical and electromagnetic compliant active BD-RIS aided communication model. We also design two new active BD-RIS architectures, namely fully- and group-connected active BD-RISs. Based on the proposed model and architecture, we investigate the active BD-RIS aided single-input single-output system and derive the closed-form optimal solution and scaling law of the signal-to-noise ratio. We further investigate the active BD-RIS aided multiple-input multiple-output system and propose an iterative algorithm based on quadratically constrained quadratic programming to maximize the spectral efficiency. Numerical results are provided and show that the active BD-RIS can achieve higher spectral efficiency than the active/passive diagonal RIS and passive BD-RIS. For example, to achieve the same spectral efficiency, the number of elements required by active BD-RIS is less than half of that required by active diagonal RIS, showing the advantages of active BD-RIS.

</details>


### [6] [Airy Beam Engineering in Near-field Communications: A Tractable Closed-Form Analysis in the Terahertz Band](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.13866)
*Wenqi Zhao,Chong Han,Emil Björnson*

Main category: eess.SP

TL;DR: 提出了一种用于太赫兹通信的闭式艾里波束设计方法，在准视距场景中通过非衍射弯曲轨迹绕过障碍物，相比传统波束成形性能显著提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 太赫兹通信需要大天线阵列，随着天线孔径扩大进入近场区域，传统的视距/非视距二分法已不足。准视距场景（视距路径部分受阻）日益普遍，传统波束成形面临挑战。现有艾里波束研究要么缺乏实用生成方案，要么依赖缺乏可解释性的学习框架。

Method: 提出闭式艾里波束设计方法，基于收发器和障碍物位置高效合成艾里波束相位剖面。通过严格的电场和轨迹解析推导，建立ULA艾里波束成形的确定性闭式设计。利用3D波前可分离性，扩展到UPA阵列，提供两种操作模式：混合聚焦-艾里模式和双艾里模式。

Result: 仿真验证了推导的轨迹方程有效性，证明所提闭式设计在准视距场景中显著优于传统波束成形方案。同时，该方法在低计算复杂度下实现了与穷举数值搜索相当的性能，并增强了物理可解释性。

Conclusion: 该闭式艾里波束设计为太赫兹通信中的准视距场景提供了高效、可解释的解决方案，利用艾里波束的非衍射和弯曲轨迹特性有效绕过障碍物，为未来无线系统提供了有前景的波束成形方法。

Abstract: Terahertz (THz) communication can offer terabit-per-second rates in future wireless systems, thanks to the ultra-wide bandwidths, but require large antenna arrays. As antenna apertures expand and we enter the near-field scenarios, the conventional binary classification of communication links as either Line-of-Sight (LoS) or Non-Line-of-Sight (NLoS) becomes insufficient. Instead, quasi-LoS scenarios, where the LoS path is partially obstructed, are increasingly prevalent, posing significant challenges for traditional LoS focusing and steering beams. The Airy beam serves as a promising alternative, utilizing its non-diffracting and curved trajectory properties to mitigate such blockages. However, while existing electromagnetics literature primarily explores their physical patterns without practical generation schemes, recent communication-oriented designs predominantly rely on learning-based frameworks lacking interpretable closed-form solutions. To address this issue, this paper investigates a closed-form Airy beam design to efficiently synthesize Airy beam phase profiles based on the positions of the transceivers and obstacles. Specifically, rigorous analytical derivations of the electric field and trajectory are presented to establish a deterministic closed-form design for ULA Airy beamforming. Leveraging 3D wavefront separability, this framework is extended to uniform planar arrays (UPAs) with two operation modes: the hybrid focusing-Airy mode and the dual Airy mode. Simulation results verify the effectiveness of our derived trajectory equations and demonstrate that the proposed closed-form design significantly outperforms conventional beamforming schemes in quasi-LoS scenarios. Furthermore, the proposed method achieves performance comparable to exhaustive numerical searches with low computational complexity and enhanced physical interpretability.

</details>


### [7] [Antenna Placement Design for Interference Exploitation in Pinching-Antenna Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.13929)
*Haoran Pang,Miaowen Wen,Fei Ji,Jun Li,Yiwei Tao*

Main category: eess.SP

TL;DR: 该论文研究了将夹持天线系统(PAS)与符号级预编码(SLP)相结合，通过联合优化波束成形和天线位置来最小化发射功率，提升无线网络性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为了满足未来无线网络对高数据速率和大规模设备部署的严格要求，需要结合PAS的灵活天线技术和SLP的干扰转换能力，进一步提升系统性能。

Method: 采用交替优化(AO)框架，将波束成形向量和PA位置系数解耦。对于给定的波束成形向量，针对PA位置制定新的目标函数，利用目标函数特性将位置优化分解为多个独立子问题，采用基于投影梯度下降(PGD)的方法在PA可行移动区域内求解次优位置系数。

Result: 通过仿真验证了PAS与SLP结合带来的性能提升，以及所提算法的有效性。

Conclusion: PAS与SLP的结合能够有效增强系统性能，所提出的联合优化算法能够实现发射功率最小化，为未来无线网络提供了一种有前景的技术方案。

Abstract: Pinching-antenna systems (PASs) have been proposed as a flexible antenna technology to fulfill the stringent requirements of high data rate and large-scale equipment deployment in future wireless networks. The principle of PA involves mapping a signal over dielectric waveguides for transmission. By adjusting the positions of pinching antennas (PAs) over the waveguides, with the aim of gain enhancement for line-of-sight links and the reduction of large-scale path loss. Symbol-level precoding (SLP) is a nonlinear precoding technique, which converts multi-user interference into constructive interference via beamforming design at symbol level. In this paper, we study the combination of SLP and PAS, leveraging the advantages of PAS to further enhance the ability of SLP to convert constructive interference. The transmit power minimization problem is formulated and solved for the multiple waveguides multiple PAs system by jointly beamforming and PAs' positions design under the SLP principle. The alternating optimization (AO) framework is applied to decouple the beamforming vector and the position coefficient of PA. For the given beamforming vectors, a new objective function is formulated with respect to the positions of the PAs. With the characteristics of the formulated objective function, the optimization problem for the position coefficients of PAs can be decomposed into multiple independent subproblems, each corresponding to a PA's position coefficient, and a projected gradient descent (PGD)-based method, constrained by the feasible movable region of each PA, is then developed to obtain the suboptimal position coefficients.
  The performance improvements achieved by the combination of PAS and SLP, as well as the effectiveness of the proposed algorithm are verified through the simulation results.

</details>


### [8] [NLOS-Aided Joint OTA Synchronization and Off-Grid Imaging for Distributed MIMO Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.13981)
*Xin Tong,Lechen Zhang,Yu Ge,Dario Tagliaferri,Henk Wymeersch*

Main category: eess.SP

TL;DR: 提出了一种用于分布式MIMO ISAC系统的NLOS辅助联合OTA同步与离网成像框架，通过闭环优化实现同步与成像的相互增强


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 分布式MIMO架构为大规模ISAC提供了高空间分辨率和鲁棒性，但实际相位相干感知面临相位同步误差和网格离散化导致的建模失配问题。现有的OTA同步方法通常将同步和感知任务分开处理，这可能导致使用多径分量进行成像时相位对齐不准确。

Method: 1. LOS辅助的粗同步建立分布式链路的初始相位相干性；2. 迭代细化阶段利用成像结果重建的NLOS分量；3. 通过一阶泰勒展开建模离网效应，将非线性离网偏移转化为具有联合稀疏反射率和偏移变量的增广线性模型；4. 将成像问题重构为结构化稀疏恢复任务，使用定制的离网近似消息传递(OG-AMP)算法求解；5. 成像和同步模块在闭环交替优化框架中耦合，改进的成像实现更精确的相位细化，反之亦然。

Result: 数值结果表明，所提出的框架在相位误差下实现了准确的同步和成像。与传统方法相比，显示出优越的鲁棒性和准确性。

Conclusion: 该论文提出的NLOS辅助联合OTA同步与离网成像框架通过闭环优化实现了同步与成像的相互增强，解决了分布式MIMO ISAC系统中的相位同步误差和网格离散化问题，显著提高了系统的鲁棒性和准确性。

Abstract: Distributed multiple-input multiple-output (MIMO) architectures enable large-scale integrated sensing and communication (ISAC) by providing high spatial resolution and robustness through spatial diversity. However, practical phase-coherent sensing is challenged by phase synchronization errors and modeling mismatch caused by grid discretization. Existing over-the-air (OTA) synchronization methods typically treat synchronization and sensing tasks separately, which may lead to inaccurate phase alignment when multipath components are used for imaging. In this paper, we propose a non-line-of-sight (NLOS)-aided joint OTA synchronization and off-grid imaging framework for distributed MIMO ISAC systems. First, a line-of-sight (LOS)-assisted coarse synchronization is performed to establish initial phase coherence across distributed links. Subsequently, an iterative refinement stage exploits reconstructed NLOS components obtained from imaging results. By modeling off-grid effects via a first-order Taylor expansion, we transform measurements with nonlinear off-grid offset into an augmented linear model with jointly sparse reflectivity and off-set variables. The imaging problem is reformulated as a structured sparse recovery task and solved using a tailored off-grid approximate message passing (OG-AMP) algorithm. The imaging and synchronization modules are coupled within a closed-loop alternative optimization framework, where improved imaging enables more accurate phase refinement, and vice versa. Numerical results show that the proposed framework achieves accurate synchronization and imaging under phase errors. Compared with conventional approaches, it shows superior robustness and accuracy.

</details>

*Mingcheng Zhu,Yu Liu,Zhiyao Luo,Tingting Zhu*

Main category: eess.SP

TL;DR: 本文综述了电子健康记录事件流建模方法，提出了统一的事件流定义和分类体系，系统回顾了从监督学习到自监督学习的训练策略，并讨论了临床应用和未来挑战。

Method: 建立统一的EHR事件流定义，提出基于事件时间、类型和值处理方式的新型分类体系，系统回顾监督学习到自监督学习的训练策略，全面讨论临床应用场景。

Result: 提出了一个清晰的事件流建模框架和分类体系，系统梳理了现有方法，为下一代医疗模型开发提供了指导，并识别了开放的关键挑战和未来研究方向。

Conclusion: 事件流表示能更好地捕捉患者旅程的精确时间结构，本文通过建立统一框架和分类体系，旨在澄清当前研究现状，指导下一代医疗人工智能模型的发展。

Abstract: The widespread adoption of electronic health records (EHRs) enables the acquisition of heterogeneous clinical data, spanning lab tests, vital signs, medications, and procedures, which offer transformative potential for artificial intelligence in healthcare. Although traditional modelling approaches have typically relied on multivariate time series, they often struggle to accommodate the inherent sparsity and irregularity of real-world clinical workflows. Consequently, research has shifted toward event stream representation, which treats patient records as continuous sequences, thereby preserving the precise temporal structure of the patient journey. However, the existing literature remains fragmented, characterised by inconsistent definitions, disparate modelling architectures, and varying training protocols. To address these gaps, this review establishes a unified definition of EHR event streams and introduces a novel taxonomy that categorises models based on their handling of event time, type, and value. We systematically review training strategies, ranging from supervised learning to self-supervised methods, and provide a comprehensive discussion of applications across clinical scenarios. Finally, we identify open critical challenges and future directions, with the aim of clarifying the current landscape and guiding the development of next-generation healthcare models.

</details>


### [10] [A Multi-Objective Learning Approach for Adaptive Waveform Selection in Integrated Sensing and Communications Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.14017)
*Ahmet Yazar,Yusuf Islam Demir,Ahmed Naeem,Seyit Karatepe*

Main category: eess.SP

TL;DR: 提出一种基于多目标学习的自适应波形选择框架，用于集成感知与通信系统，通过机器学习模型学习网络条件与帕累托最优波形集之间的映射关系，实现动态网络状态下的快速波形选择。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在6G集成感知与通信系统中，通信和感知功能存在固有的性能冲突，特别是在异构网络中用户需求多样（仅感知、仅通信或联合服务）。选择满足多样化服务需求的波形成为一个具有挑战性的多目标决策问题。

Method: 开发仿真驱动的评估框架，评估多个波形候选方案在通信、感知和联合性能指标上的表现；在多维目标空间中识别帕累托最优候选波形；生成包含不同用户需求分布和信道条件的数据集，基于帕累托支配构建多标签目标；训练机器学习模型学习网络条件与帕累托最优波形集之间的映射关系。

Conclusion: 该多目标学习框架为ISAC系统中的自适应波形选择提供了有效解决方案，能够应对通信与感知之间的性能冲突，适应动态网络状态和多样化用户需求，具有重要的实际应用价值。

Abstract: Integrated Sensing and Communications (ISAC) has emerged as a key enabler for sixth generation (6G) wireless systems by jointly supporting data transmission and environmental awareness within a unified framework. However, communication and sensing functionalities impose inherently conflicting performance requirements, particularly in heterogeneous networks where users may demand sensing only, communication only, or joint services. Selecting a waveform that satisfies diverse service demands therefore becomes a challenging multi objective decision problem. In this paper, a multi objective learning approach for adaptive waveform selection in ISAC systems is proposed. A simulation driven evaluation framework is developed to assess multiple waveform candidates across communication, sensing, and joint performance metrics. Instead of enforcing scalar utility aggregation, waveform performance is represented in a multi dimensional objective space where Pareto optimal candidates are identified for each scenario. A dataset is generated by varying user demand distributions and channel conditions, and multi-label targets are constructed based on Pareto dominance. Machine learning models are trained to learn the mapping between network conditions and Pareto optimal waveform sets, enabling fast waveform selection under dynamic network states. Simulation results demonstrate that the proposed framework effectively adapts waveform selection to heterogeneous service requirements while preserving sensing communication trade offs, providing a forward-looking perspective for 6G and beyond ISAC deployments.

</details>

*Giuseppe Pettanice,Andrea Abrardo,Alberto Toccafondi,Marco Di Renzo*

Main category: eess.SP

TL;DR: 提出基于散射参数多端口网络表示的堆叠智能超表面建模与优化框架，考虑实际电路特性与端口互耦，相比简化模型提供更准确的性能预测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有SIM优化框架大多依赖简化的信道抽象，忽略了多端口耦合、电路损耗和非理想硬件行为等关键电磁效应，导致性能预测不准确。

Method: 采用基于散射参数的多端口网络表示法建立SIM模型，该公式化方法能捕捉实际电路特性和端口间互耦，同时保持可优化性，并通过电磁仿真验证模型。

Result: 电磁仿真验证了模型有效性，数值结果表明该框架能准确预测性能，在通信和传感场景下实现实际电磁条件下的有效SIM配置设计。

Conclusion: 提出的多端口网络建模框架能更真实地反映SIM的电磁特性，为实际硬件条件下的SIM优化设计提供了有效工具，弥补了简化模型的不足。

Abstract: Stacked intelligent metasurfaces (SIMs) extend the concept of reconfigurable intelligent surfaces by cascading multiple programmable layers, enabling advanced electromagnetic wave transformations for communication and sensing applications. However, most existing optimization frameworks rely on simplified channel abstractions that may overlook key electromagnetic effects such as multiport coupling, circuit losses, and non-ideal hardware behavior. In this paper, we develop a modeling and optimization framework for SIMs based on a multiport network representation using scattering parameters. The proposed formulation captures realistic circuit characteristics and mutual interactions among SIM ports while remaining amenable to optimization. The resulting models are validated through electromagnetic simulations, enabling a systematic comparison between idealized and practical SIM configurations. Numerical results for communication and sensing scenarios confirm that the proposed framework provides accurate performance predictions and enables the effective design of SIM configurations under realistic electromagnetic conditions.

</details>


### [12] [AI/ML for mobile networks: Current status in Rel. 19 and challenges ahead](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.14317)
*Yuan Gao,Xinyi Wu,Jun Jiang,Bintao Hu,Jianbo Du,Qiang Ye,Shunqing Zhang,F. Richard Yu,Shugong Xu*

Main category: eess.SP

TL;DR: 本文全面回顾了3GPP在移动网络中AI/ML标准化的现状，包括框架、用例和评估指标，并通过CSI反馈案例展示了预训练-微调范式的优势，指出了数据集准备、泛化评估等关键挑战。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI/ML被认为是6G移动网络的关键使能技术，3GPP已启动相关标准化工作，但目前缺乏对移动网络中AI/ML标准化现状和挑战的全面综述。

Method: 提供3GPP在移动网络中AI/ML标准化的全面回顾，包括通用AI/ML框架、代表性用例（CSI反馈、波束管理和定位）及相应评估指标。以CSI反馈为案例研究，使用测试数据集2，比较预训练-微调范式与直接训练的差异。

Result: 预训练-微调范式（使用数据集1预训练，数据集2微调）优于直接在数据集2上训练。Transformer模型通过微调获得最高性能提升，显示出在大规模FLOPs下的强大泛化潜力。

Conclusion: 本文填补了移动网络中AI/ML标准化现状综述的空白，强调了数据集准备、泛化评估和基线模型选择等关键挑战，并指出了未来研究方向。

Abstract: The transformative power of artificial intelligence (AI) and machine learning (ML) is recognized as a key enabler for sixth generation (6G) mobile networks by both academia and industry. Research on AI/ML in mobile networks has been ongoing for years, and the 3rd generation partnership project (3GPP) launched standardization efforts to integrate AI into mobile networks. However, a comprehensive review of the current status and challenges of the standardization of AI/ML for mobile networks is still missing. To this end, we provided a comprehensive review of the standardization efforts by 3GPP on AI/ML for mobile networks. This includes an overview of the general AI/ML framework, representative use cases (i.e., CSI feedback, beam management and positioning), and corresponding evaluation matrices. We emphasized the key research challenges on dataset preparation, generalization evaluation and baseline AI/ML models selection. Using CSI feedback as a case study, given the test dataset 2, we demonstrated that the pre-training-fine-tuning paradigm (i.e., pre-training using dataset 1 and fine-tuning using dataset 2) outperforms training on dataset 2. Moreover, we observed the highest performance enhancements in Transformer-based models through fine-tuning, showing its great generalization potential at large floating-point operations (FLOPs). Finally, we outlined future research directions for the application of AI/ML in mobile networks.

</details>


### [13] [Clutter-Resilient ISAC for Low-Altitude Wireless Networks: A 5G Base Station-Compatible Protocol, Waveform, and Prototype](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.14351)
*Jie Wang,Zhen Du,Ying Wang,Weijie Yuan,Fan Liu,Xingdong Liang,Yong Zeng*

Main category: eess.SP

TL;DR: 5G-A地面基站升级为集成感知通信系统，用于低空无线网络中弱慢无人机的检测与跟踪，在1公里距离有效追踪目标，仅带来1.2%下行速率损失。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 低空无线网络需要支持新兴服务，将5G地面基站升级为具有广覆盖、低成本、高精度和良好频谱兼容性的主动感知系统是迫切需求，但面临弱慢无人机检测跟踪的挑战。

Method: 提出综合框架实现5G-A地面基站升级，包括ISAC波形设计、高杂噪比下的稳健杂波消除、无人机与杂波的高效多普勒分离等关键技术解决方案。

Result: 外场实验表明，开发的5G-A地面基站能在超过1公里距离有效跟踪弱慢目标，相对于商用5G-A地面基站仅产生1.2%的下行速率损失。

Conclusion: 通过综合框架将5G-A地面基站升级为集成感知通信系统，成功解决了低空无线网络中弱慢无人机检测跟踪的关键挑战，实现了感知与通信的良好平衡。

Abstract: Integrated sensing and communications (ISAC) has been envisioned as a promising solution to support emerging services in low-altitude wireless networks (LAWNs), where upgrading 5G ground base stations (GBS) toward new active sensing systems with wide coverage, low cost, high accuracy, and favorable spectrum compatibility, is strongly desired. However, such an evolution faces several critical challenges, particularly in the detection and tracking of weak and slow unmanned aerial vehicles (UAVs). These challenges include ISAC waveform design, clutter cancellation resilient to high clutter-to-noise ratios (CNRs), and efficient Doppler separation between UAVs and clutter. To that end, we summarize potential solutions and raise a comprehensive framework on implementing the 5Gadvanced (5G-A) GBS. Outfield experiments demonstrate that the developed 5G-A GBS can effectively track weak and slow targets at distances exceeding 1 kilometer, while incurring only a 1.2% downlink rate loss relative to commercial 5G-A GBS.

</details>


### [14] [Geometric Framework for Robust Order Detection in Delay-Coordinates Dynamic Mode Decomposition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.14383)
*Yoav Harris,Hadas Benisty,Ronen Talmon*

Main category: eess.SP

TL;DR: 提出基于子空间几何的DC-DMD模型阶数检测和模式选择新方法，通过几何残差和KV结构一致性区分真实模式与虚假模式


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: DC-DMD在从高维时间序列提取相干时空模式时面临核心挑战：如何区分由噪声和阶数过估计引起的虚假模式与有动态意义的真实模式

Method: 1) 基于子空间几何的方法：真实模式集中在低维信号子空间内，虚假模式在该子空间外有不可忽略分量；2) 基于KV结构的方法：所有模式呈现Kronecker-Vandermonde结构，真实模式更符合该结构；两种方法通过几何残差关联

Result: 提出的几何和结构方法在噪声水平、谱分离、阻尼机制和嵌入长度等条件下，比现有基线方法实现了更鲁棒和准确的阶数检测与模式选择

Conclusion: DC-DMD中的模型阶数检测和模式选择本质上是子空间几何问题，提出的几何框架为区分真实与虚假模式提供了理论基础和实用标准

Abstract: Delay-coordinates dynamic mode decomposition (DC-DMD) is widely used to extract coherent spatiotemporal modes from high-dimensional time series. A central challenge is distinguishing dynamically meaningful modes from spurious modes induced by noise and order overestimation. We show that model order detection and mode selection in DC-DMD are fundamentally problems of subspace geometry. Specifically, true modes are characterized by concentration within a low-dimensional signal subspace, whereas spurious modes necessarily retain non-negligible components outside any moderate overestimate of that subspace. This geometric distinction yields a perturbation-robust definition of true and spurious modes and yields fully data-driven selection criteria. This geometric framework leads to two complementary data-driven selection criteria. The first is derived directly from the geometric distinction and uses a data-driven proxy of the signal-subspace to compute a residual score. The second arises from a new operator-theoretic analysis of delay embedding. Using a block-companion formulation, we show that all modes exhibit a Kronecker-Vandermonde (KV) structure induced by the delay-coordinates, and true modes are distinguished by the degree to which they conform to it. Importantly, we also show that this deviation is governed precisely by the geometric residual. In addition, our analysis provides a principled explanation for the empirical behavior of magnitude- and norm-based heuristics, clarifying when and why they fail under delay-coordinates. Extensive numerical experiments confirm the theoretical predictions and demonstrate that the proposed geometric and structure-based methods achieve robust and accurate order detection and mode selection, consistently better than existing baselines across noise levels, spectral separations, damping regimes, and embedding lengths.

</details>


### [15] [Near-Field Channel Estimation for mmWave/THz Communications with Extremely Large-Scale UPAs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.14437)
*Yiming Chen,Hongwei Wang,Lingxiang Li,Zhi Chen*

Main category: eess.SP

TL;DR: 提出一种针对均匀平面阵列近场信道估计的二维块稀疏方法，通过构建改进的2D-DFT字典和2D-PCSBL算法，相比现有方法性能更优且计算复杂度相当。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 毫米波/太赫兹通信中广泛使用极大规模天线阵列，但瑞利距离延伸至数十甚至数百米，需要考虑近场信道模型。现有方法主要针对均匀线性阵列设计，在均匀平面阵列场景下性能有限或无法直接扩展。

Method: 将UPA近场信道重构为两个ULA近场信道的外积，构建改进的二维DFT字典，证明UPA近场信道具有二维块稀疏结构，并采用二维模式耦合稀疏贝叶斯学习算法进行信道估计。

Result: 仿真结果表明，所提方法在保持相当计算复杂度的同时，优于传统的现有方法。

Conclusion: 通过利用UPA近场信道的特定稀疏结构，提出的二维块稀疏方法能有效解决UPA近场信道估计问题，为极大规模天线阵列通信系统提供更好的信道估计方案。

Abstract: Extremely large antenna arrays (ELAAs) are widely adopted in mmWave/THz communications to compensate for the severe path loss, wherein the channel estimation remains a significant challenge since the Rayleigh distance of ELAAs stretches to tens or even hundreds of meters and the near-field channel model should be considered. Existing polar-domain based methods and block-sparse based methods are originally devised for Uniform Linear Arrays (ULAs) near-field channel estimation. The polar-domain based method can be applied to Uniform Planar Arrays (UPAs), but it behaves plain since it ignores the specific sparsity structure of the UPA near-field channels. Meanwhile, the block-sparse based method cannot be extended to the UPA scenarios directly. To address these issues, we first reformulate the original UPA near-field channel as an outer product of two ULA near-field channels and we construct a modified two-dimensional DFT (2D-DFT) dictionary for it. With the proposed dictionary, we further prove that the UPA near-field channel admits a 2D block-sparse structure. Leveraging this specific sparse structure, we solve the channel estimation problem with the 2D Pattern-Coupled Sparse Bayesian Learning (2D-PCSBL) algorithm. Simulation results show that the proposed approach outperforms conventional existing methods while maintaining a comparable computational complexity.

</details>


### [16] [A Decoupling-based Approach for Signature Estimation of Wideband XL MIMO-FMCW Radars](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.14542)
*Chandrashekhar Rai,Dibbendu Roy,Debarati Sen*

Main category: eess.SP

TL;DR: 提出一种基于解耦的目标检测与参数估计框架，用于MIMO FMCW雷达，通过将二维联合估计转化为解耦的序列频率估计，解决空间宽带效应引起的目标响应重叠问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代雷达使用宽带信号和超大MIMO阵列时，空间延迟与距离分辨率相当，导致空间宽带效应，引起距离偏移、波束偏移和距离-角度耦合等问题，使目标响应在距离-角度域扩散，导致物理分离的目标重叠和相互掩盖。

Method: 将联合距离-角度估计问题重新表述为解耦的序列频率估计问题，通过连续的一维超分辨率估计实现二维估计。具体采用正交匹配追踪进行基于稀疏恢复的距离和角度估计，并将该解耦策略扩展到空间宽带超大MIMO FMCW雷达系统。

Result: 仿真结果表明，该方法能准确检测多个目标，在存在空间宽带效应的情况下成功解析重叠的目标响应，性能优于传统的傅里叶变换和基于聚类的方法。

Conclusion: 提出的解耦框架有效解决了空间宽带效应引起的目标响应重叠问题，为超大MIMO FMCW雷达系统提供了可靠的目标检测和分离能力。

Abstract: Modern radars employing wideband signals and extremely large (XL) multiple-input multiple-output (MIMO) arrays can significantly improve range and angular resolution. However, when large bandwidth and array aperture are used simultaneously, the spatial delay across the array becomes comparable to the radar range resolution, leading to the spatial wideband effect (SWE). The SWE introduces several distortions including range migration (range squint), beam squint, and range-angle coupling (RAC), which spread the target response in the range-angle domain and may cause physically separated targets to overlap and mask each other. In this work, we propose a decoupling-based target detection and parameter estimation framework for MIMO frequency modulated continuous wave (FMCW) radar. The proposed method reformulates the joint range-angle estimation problem as a decoupled sequential frequency estimation problem, where the two-dimensional (2D) estimation is carried out through successive one-dimensional (1D) super-resolution estimations. Specifically, we employ orthogonal matching pursuit (OMP) to perform sparse recovery-based range and angle estimation with high resolution. The proposed decoupling strategy is further extended to spatial wideband XL-MIMO FMCW radar systems, enabling reliable detection and separation of targets even when their responses overlap due to severe RAC. Simulation results demonstrate that the proposed approach accurately detects multiple targets and successfully resolves overlapping target responses in the presence of SWE, outperforming conventional Fourier transform and clustering-based methods.

</details>


### [17] [Two-Stage Heterogeneous Graph Neural Network for RIS-Aided Physical-Layer Security](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.14655)
*Zihan Song,Yang Lu,Wei Chen,Bo Ai,Zhiguo Ding,Arumugam Nallanathan*

Main category: eess.SP

TL;DR: 提出两阶段异构图神经网络方法，用于最大化RIS辅助MISO系统的保密能效，相比现有方法显著降低计算时间且性能损失小。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统凸优化方法在RIS辅助物理层安全系统中计算复杂度高，难以扩展到大规模网络。需要设计能够有效处理异构节点关系、具有良好可扩展性的高效算法。

Method: 采用两阶段异构图神经网络：第一阶段将系统建模为包含RIS反射单元、合法用户和窃听者的二分图，生成RIS相移矩阵；第二阶段建模为包含合法用户和窃听者的全连接图，生成波束赋形和人工噪声向量。两阶段均集成多头注意力机制，第二阶段包含两种输出方法。

Result: 提出的两阶段HGNN在RIS辅助物理层安全场景中优于现有GNN方法。相比凸优化算法，平均运行时间减少三个数量级，性能损失小于4%。通过大量仿真验证了方法的可扩展性。

Conclusion: 两阶段异构图神经网络为RIS辅助物理层安全系统提供了一种高效、可扩展的解决方案，在保持高性能的同时大幅降低计算复杂度，适用于大规模网络部署。

Abstract: This paper investigates physical-layer security (PLS) enabled by graph neural networks (GNNs). We propose a two-stage heterogeneous GNN (HGNN) to maximize the secrecy energy efficiency (SEE) of a reconfigurable intelligent surface (RIS)-assisted multi-input-single-output (MISO) system that serves multiple legitimate users (LUs) and eavesdroppers (Eves). The first stage formulates the system as a bipartite graph involving three types of nodes-RIS reflecting elements, LUs, and Eves-with the goal of generating the RIS phase shift matrix. The second stage models the system as a fully connected graph with two types of nodes (LUs and Eves), aiming to produce beamforming and artificial noise (AN) vectors. Both stages adopt an HGNN integrated with a multi-head attention mechanism, and the second stage incorporates two output methods: beam-direct and model-based approaches. The two-stage HGNN is trained in an unsupervised manner and designed to scale with the number of RIS reflecting elements, LUs, and Eves. Numerical results demonstrate that the proposed two-stage HGNN outperforms state-of-the-art GNNs in RIS-aided PLS scenarios. Compared with convex optimization algorithms, it reduces the average running time by three orders of magnitude with a performance loss of less than $4\%$. Additionally, the scalability of the two-stage HGNN is validated through extensive simulations.

</details>

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统远场ISAC系统在有限带宽下识别精度受限，近场球面波提供了距离相关的空间特征，可以更高效地利用带宽资源实现高精度目标识别

Method: 提出STF Transformer框架：1) 轻量级空间编码器提取天线阵列特征；2) 时空频Transformer头融合特征，使用位置嵌入建模时间动态和跨子载波相关性

Result: 仿真结果表明，即使在有限带宽资源下，该框架也能实现强大的目标识别性能，验证了近场ISAC系统的有效性

Conclusion: 近场ISAC系统结合STF Transformer框架能够高效利用带宽实现高精度目标识别，为资源受限的感知通信一体化系统提供了有效解决方案

Abstract: A target recognition framework relying on near-field integrated sensing and communication (ISAC) systems is proposed. By exploiting the distance-dependent spatial signatures provided by the near-field spherical wavefront, high-accuracy sensing is realized in a bandwidth-efficient manner. A spatio--temporal--frequency (STF) transformer framework is introduced for target recognition using electromagnetic features found in the wireless channel response. In particular, a lightweight spatial encoder is employed to extract features from the antenna array for each frame and subcarrier. These features are then fused by a time-frequency transformer head with positional embeddings to model temporal dynamics and cross-subcarrier correlations. Simulation results demonstrate that strong target recognition performance can be achieved even with limited bandwidth resources.

</details>


### [19] [Flag-Preamble-Based Delay-Doppler Channel Estimation for Next-Evolution Waveforms](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.14875)
*Yuan Liu,Yilin Qi,Caiqin Li,Haoran Yin,Yanqun Tang,Yao Ge*

Main category: eess.SP

TL;DR: 提出一种结合Flag序列优化和信道估计算法的鲁棒信道估计方案，通过候选选择策略和全局最小二乘优化提升性能


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 下一代演进波形需要精确的时延-多普勒信道估计以实现可靠信号检测，传统贪婪估计算法存在鲁棒性问题

Method: 1) 使用自适应加速并行主最小化算法优化Flag序列；2) 提出候选选择策略抑制虚假旁瓣峰值；3) 引入全局最小二乘优化阶段消除旁瓣掩蔽效应导致的误差传播

Result: 数值结果表明所提方案显著优于传统现有算法，达到期望的估计精度

Conclusion: 提出的鲁棒信道估计算法通过Flag序列优化和两级增强策略，有效解决了传统方法的鲁棒性问题，为下一代演进波形提供了可靠的信道估计方案

Abstract: Accurate delay-Doppler channel estimation is critical for next-evolution waveforms (NEWs) to enable reliable signal detection. This paper proposes a robust channel estimation algorithm that integrates Flag sequences optimized via an adaptive accelerated parallel majorization-minimization (AP-MM) algorithm with a proposed channel estimation algorithm. To enable efficient, low-complexity parameter extraction and further overcome the robustness issues of conventional greedy estimation, we introduce two key enhancements, i.e., a candidate selection strategy to mitigate spurious sidelobe peaks, and a global least squares (LS) refinement stage to eliminate error propagation caused by sidelobe masking effects. Numerical results demonstrate that the proposed scheme significantly outperforms traditional existing algorithms, achieving the desired estimation accuracy.

</details>


### [20] [Integrated Channel Sounding and Communication: Requirements, Architecture, Challenges, and Key Technologies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.14912)
*Nanhao Zhou,Chao Zou,Yu Zhou,Yanqun Tang,Xiaoying Zhang,Haoran Yin,Xuefeng Yin,Yuxiang Zhang,Dan Fei,Fan Jiang*

Main category: eess.SP

TL;DR: 提出集成信道探测与通信（ICSC）框架，通过深度集成探测与通信功能，实现无线通信系统中动态信道特性的实时高效获取，支持细粒度的场景特定测量，并利用AI技术实时识别信道条件并自适应调整波形参数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 空天地海一体化网络（SAGSIN）具有多样化服务应用和扩展频谱操作的特点，对高精度信道模型提出了更高要求。传统信道探测方法受限于通用化测量、跨频段一致性不足和实时适应性差等问题，无法满足SAGSIN对场景特定和高精度信道建模的需求。

Method: 提出集成信道探测与通信（ICSC）技术框架，通过深度集成探测与通信功能，在通信过程中实时高效获取动态信道特性。利用人工智能技术实时识别信道条件，根据场景变化自适应调整波形参数。开发了集成验证系统（IVS）来验证框架可行性。

Result: ICSC框架能够支持细粒度的站点和场景特定测量，通过AI技术增强通信性能。集成验证系统（IVS）的开发证明了该框架的可行性。文章还深入分析了ICSC的潜在应用机会。

Conclusion: ICSC为解决SAGSIN中高精度信道建模挑战提供了创新解决方案，通过集成探测与通信功能实现了实时动态信道特性获取和自适应优化。未来需要进一步探索其发展方向并解决剩余挑战。

Abstract: Channel models are essential for the design, evaluation, and optimization of wireless communication systems. The emerging space-air-ground-sea integrated network (SAGSIN), characterized by diverse service applications and extended-spectrum operations, places even greater demands on highly accurate channel models. However, conventional channel sounding is limited by generalized measurement campaigns, inadequate cross-band consistency, and insufficient real-time adaptability, making it unable to meet the needs of SAGSIN for scenario-specific and high-precision channel modeling. To address this challenge, we propose a novel technological framework, termed integrated channel sounding and communication (ICSC). By deeply integrating sounding and communication, the ICSC enables efficient and real-time acquisition of dynamic channel characteristics during communication processes, supporting fine-grained site- and scenario-specific measurements. Furthermore, leveraging artificial intelligence techniques, ICSC can identify channel conditions and adapt waveform parameters in real-time according to scenario variations, which in turn enhances communication performance. This article first introduces the fundamental principles of the ICSC framework, elaborates on its core concepts and key advantages, and demonstrates its feasibility through the development of an integrated verification system (IVS). Subsequently, the potential applications and opportunities of the ICSC are analyzed in depth, followed by a discussion of its future development directions and remaining challenges.

</details>


### [21] [RF-Fencing: A Novel RIS-Based Service for Proactive Covert Communications](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.14943)
*Alexandros I. Papadopoulos,Dimitrios Tyrovolas,Alexandros Pitilakis,Panagiotis D. Diamantoulakis,Antonios Lalas,Konstantinos Votis,Nikolaos V. Kantartzis,Sotiris Ioannidis,Christos Liaskos*

Main category: eess.SP

TL;DR: RF-Fencing是一种基于可重构智能表面的新型无线环境服务，通过空间选择性控制实现信号抑制与合法用户连接的平衡，SHIELD算法通过预编译码本与实时优化实现多RIS单元协同。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 可编程无线环境作为下一代网络的变革性范式，需要实现对电磁传播的确定性控制以提升性能和安全性。当前需要一种能够同时抑制不必要信号暴露并维持合法用户稳健连接的空间选择性控制方案。

Method: 提出RF-Fencing服务框架，开发SHIELD轻量级可扩展算法，通过复用预编译码本条目与实时低复杂度优化来协调多个RIS单元，实现远场方向性控制和近场静区创建。

Result: SHIELD在不同频率、RIS配置和部署场景下有效平衡主动隐蔽通信与服务交付，动态管理多个信号抑制和传输区域，实现电磁静区创建且对周围区域影响最小。

Conclusion: RF-Fencing为未来6G网络中隐私保护和自适应无线环境提供了实用的RIS基础，SHIELD算法展示了在可编程无线环境中实现空间选择性控制的可行性。

Abstract: Programmable wireless environments (PWEs), empowered by reconfigurable intelligent surfaces (RISes), have emerged as a transformative paradigm for next-generation networks, enabling deterministic control over electromagnetic (EM) propagation to enhance both performance and security. In this work, we introduce RF-Fencing, a novel RIS-enabled PWE service that enforces spatially selective control over wireless transmissions, simultaneously suppressing unwanted signal exposure while sustaining robust connectivity for legitimate users. To realize this vision, we develop SHIELD, a lightweight and scalable algorithm that orchestrates multiple RIS units by multiplexing precompiled codebook entries with real-time, low-complexity optimization. Through extensive evaluations across diverse frequencies, RIS configurations, and deployment scenarios, SHIELD demonstrates both far-field directional control and near-field quiet-zone creation, thereby enhancing network security. Our findings reveal that SHIELD effectively balances proactive covert communication with service delivery by dynamically managing multiple signal suppression and delivery areas, while enabling the realization of EM quiet zones with minimal impact on surrounding regions, ultimately establishing RF-Fencing as a practical RIS-based foundation for privacy-preserving and adaptive wireless environments in future 6G networks.

</details>


### [22] [Cyclic Delay-Doppler Shift: A Simple Transmit Diversity Technique for Ultra-Reliable Communications in Doubly Selective Channels](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.14959)
*Haoran Yin,Yu Zhou,Yanqun Tang,Di Zhang,Chi Zhang,Xizhang Wei,Jiaojiao Xiong,Fan Liu,Marwa Chafii,Mérouane Debbah*

Main category: eess.SP

TL;DR: 提出了一种称为循环延迟-多普勒偏移(CDDS)的简单发射分集技术，用于AFDM和OTFS波形，在双选择性信道中实现超可靠通信。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AFDM和OTFS是两种有前景的高级波形，适用于高移动性场景的可靠通信。需要一种简单有效的发射分集技术来提高这些波形在双选择性信道中的可靠性。

Method: 提出了两种CDDS方案：调制域CDDS(MD-CDDS)和时域CDDS(TD-CDDS)，分别在调制前和调制后在发射端执行循环延迟-多普勒偏移。通过将发射向量与精心设计的预编码矩阵相乘实现，该矩阵本质上是稀疏的相位补偿置换矩阵。

Result: 理论分析和数值模拟证明，当采用适当的CDDS步长时，CDDS可以为MIMO-AFDM和MIMO-OTFS提供最优的发射分集增益。相比传统发射分集技术，CDDS具有更低的信道估计开销、实现复杂度和信号处理延迟。

Conclusion: CDDS是一种简单高效的发射分集技术，特别适用于高移动性场景中的超可靠通信，为AFDM和OTFS波形提供了优越的性能优势。

Abstract: Affine frequency division multiplexing (AFDM) and orthogonal time frequency space (OTFS) are two promising advanced waveforms proposed for reliable communications in high-mobility scenarios. In this paper, we introduce a simple transmit diversity technique, termed cyclic delay-Doppler shift (CDDS), for these two advanced waveforms to achieve ultra-reliable communications in doubly selective channels (DSCs). Two simple CDDS schemes, named modulation-domain CDDS (MD-CDDS) and time-domain CDDS (TD-CDDS), are proposed, which perform CDDS in advance at the transmitter before and after the modulation, respectively. We demonstrate that both of the two proposed CDDS schemes can be implemented efficiently and flexibly by multiplying the transmit vector with a well-designed precoding matrix, which is nothing but a sparse phase-compensated permutation matrix. Moreover, we theoretically and numerically prove that CDDS can provide MIMO-AFDM and MIMO-OTFS with optimal transmit diversity gain when a proper CDDS step is adopted. Compared to the conventional transmit diversity techniques, the proposed CDDS scheme enjoys the advantages of lower channel estimation overhead, implementation complexity, and signal processing latency, making it particularly suitable for ultra-reliable communications in high-mobility scenarios.

</details>


### [23] [Generative Semantic HARQ: Latent-Space Text Retransmission and Combining](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.15068)
*Bin Han,Yulin Hu,Hans D. Schotten*

Main category: eess.SP

TL;DR: 提出基于Transformer-VAE的语义级HARQ框架，通过单一模型生成增量知识，无需专门协议设计，结合软质量估计与合并策略提升文本语义通信可靠性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 语义通信关注意义而非原始比特传输，但语义级的可靠性仍是开放挑战。现有方法在语义层面的错误恢复能力有限，需要新的重传机制来保证语义通信的可靠性。

Method: 提出语义级混合自动重传请求框架，使用Transformer-VAE编解码器作为传统协议栈的轻量覆盖层。随机编码器在重传中生成多样潜在表示，提供增量知识。接收端采用软质量估计器触发重传，质量感知合并器在一致潜在空间内合并接收到的潜在向量。

Result: 系统评估了六种语义质量指标和四种软合并策略，在混合系统偏差与加性噪声的语义失真下，加权平均或MRC启发式合并结合基于自一致性的HARQ触发策略表现最佳。

Conclusion: 提出的语义级HARQ框架通过单一模型实现增量知识传输，无需专门协议设计，结合适当的合并策略和质量触发机制，能有效提升文本语义通信的可靠性。

Abstract: Semantic communication conveys meaning rather than raw bits, but reliability at the semantic level remains an open challenge. We propose a semantic-level hybrid automatic repeat request (HARQ) framework for text communication, in which a Transformer-variational autoencoder (VAE) codec operates as a lightweight overlay on the conventional protocol stack. The stochastic encoder inherently generates diverse latent representations across retransmissions-providing incremental knowledge (IK) from a single model without dedicated protocol design. On the receiver side, a soft quality estimator triggers retransmissions and a quality-aware combiner merges the received latent vectors within a consistent latent space. We systematically benchmark six semantic quality metrics and four soft combining strategies under hybrid semantic distortion that mixes systematic bias with additive noise. The results suggest combining Weighted-Average or MRC-Inspired combining with self-consistency-based HARQ triggering for the best performance.

</details>


### [24] [Beam Prediction Based on Multimodal Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.15093)
*Tianhao Mao,Le Liang,Jie Yang,Xiao Li,Shi Jin*

Main category: eess.SP

TL;DR: 提出基于多模态大语言模型的波束预测框架，利用RGB图像和LiDAR点云等传感器数据，通过专门设计的模态编码器和波束引导注意力机制，在动态环境中实现鲁棒的多模态特征融合，显著提升波束预测精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 准确的波束预测是下一代无线通信系统的关键使能技术。传统方法依赖理想的出发角知识，而实际动态环境中传感器提供的上下文信息（如天气、交通状况）对波束预测至关重要，但现有方法在多模态融合方面存在挑战。

Method: 1. 设计专门的多模态编码器处理RGB图像和LiDAR点云数据；2. 提出波束引导注意力掩码机制；3. 采用高频时序对齐策略；4. 构建大规模多模态通信数据集Multimodal-Wireless，包含多种天气和交通条件下的高保真射线追踪标签。

Result: 实验结果表明：1. 显著减少对理想出发角知识的依赖；2. 在波束精度和通信性能方面持续优于最先进的多模态LLM波束预测方法；3. 平均Top-1准确率达到80.8%；4. 平均归一化增益达到89.1%。

Conclusion: 提出的多模态LLM波束预测框架通过有效的模态融合机制，在动态环境中实现了鲁棒的波束预测性能，为下一代无线通信系统提供了重要的技术支撑，特别是在减少对理想信道状态信息依赖方面表现出色。

Abstract: Accurate beam prediction is a key enabler for next-generation wireless communication systems. In this paper, we propose a multimodal large language model (LLM)-based beam prediction framework that effectively utilizes contextual information, provided by sensory data including RGB camera images and LiDAR point clouds. To effectively fuse heterogeneous modalities, we design specialized modality encoders together with a beam-guided attention masking mechanism and a high-frequency temporal alignment strategy, enabling robust cross-modal feature integration under dynamic environments. Furthermore, we construct a large-scale multimodal dataset for communication, named Multimodal-Wireless, which covers diverse weather and traffic conditions with high-fidelity ray-tracing labels. Extensive simulation results demonstrate that the proposed approach significantly reduces the reliance on oracle angle-of-departure knowledge and consistently outperforms state-of-the-art multimodal LLM-based beam prediction methods in terms of beam accuracy and communication performance, improving the average Top-1 accuracy to 80.8% and the average normalized gain to 89.1%.

</details>


### [25] [Dual-Domain Sparse Adaptive Filtering: Exploiting Error Memory for Improved Performance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.15105)
*Mohammad Salman,Hadi Zayyani,Felipe A. P. de Figueiredo,Hasan Abu Hilal,Mostafa Rashdan*

Main category: eess.SP

TL;DR: 提出双域稀疏自适应滤波器(DD-SAF)，通过同时考虑系数大小和误差记忆向量，在稀疏系统识别中更早识别活跃系数，避免对活跃系数的错误惩罚，从而加速收敛并降低稳态误差。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统零吸引自适应滤波器仅根据系数当前大小决定惩罚哪些系数，导致在早期适应阶段，那些最终会变大的活跃系数因为初始值小而受到惩罚，这反而阻碍了它们的收敛速度。

Method: 提出双域方法：除了跟踪系数大小外，引入误差记忆向量来监测每个系数随时间对适应误差的持续贡献。结合这两种视角，DD-SAF能够早期识别活跃系数并相应消除惩罚。

Result: 理论分析表明DD-SAF保持与标准LMS相同的稳定性，同时比现有方法具有更好的稳态性能。仿真显示DD-SAF在稀疏系统识别中比标准LMS和RZA-LMS收敛更快且/或达到更低的均方偏差。

Conclusion: DD-SAF通过双域方法有效解决了传统稀疏自适应滤波器中早期收敛阶段对活跃系数的错误惩罚问题，实现了更快的收敛速度和更好的稳态性能。

Abstract: Many signal processing applications such as acoustic echo cancellation and wireless channel estimation require identifying systems where only a small fraction of coefficients are actually active, i.e. sparse systems. Zero-attracting adaptive filters tackle this by adding a penalty that pulls inactive coefficients toward zero, speeding up convergence. However, these algorithms determine which coefficients to penalize based solely on their current size. This creates a problem during early adaptation since active coefficients that should eventually grow large start out small, making them look identical to truly inactive coefficients. The algorithm ends up applying strong penalties to the very coefficients it needs to develop, slowing down the initial convergence. This paper provides a solution to this problem by introducing a dual-domain approach that looks at coefficients from two perspectives simultaneously. Beyond just tracking coefficient magnitude, we introduce an error-memory vector that monitors how persistently each coefficient contributes to the adaptation error over time. If a coefficient keeps showing up in the error signal, it is probably active even if it is still small. By combining both views, the proposed dual-domain sparse adaptive filter (DD-SAF) can identify active coefficients early and eliminate penalties accordingly. Moreover, complete theoretical analysis is derived. The analysis shows that DD-SAF maintains the same stability properties as standard least-mean-square (LMS) while achieves provably better steady-state performance than existing methods. Simulations demonstrate that the DD-SAF converges to the steady-state faster and/or convergences to a lower mean-square-deviation (MSD) than the standard LMS and the reweighted zero-attracting LMS (RZA-LMS) algorithms for sparse system identification settings.

</details>


### [26] [RIS-Aided RSMA Improves the Latency vs. Energy Trade-off in the Finite Block Length MIMO Downlink](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.15234)
*Mohammad Soleymani,Bruno Clerckx,Robert Schober,Lajos Hanzo*

Main category: eess.SP

TL;DR: RIS辅助的RSMA MU-MIMO系统能同时降低延迟并提高能效，相比SDMA可将延迟降至13%，且RIS与RSMA协同作用显著，特别适合URLLC场景


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究如何在多用户MIMO系统中同时最小化延迟并提高能效，特别关注RIS辅助的RSMA技术，以支持URLLC等对延迟和可靠性要求严格的应用场景

Method: 采用可重构智能表面辅助的速率分割多址技术，在多用户MIMO下行链路中协同优化延迟和能效，并与传统空分多址技术进行对比分析

Result: RSMA相比SDMA能将延迟降低至13%，显著提高能效；RIS与RSMA协同作用明显，而单独使用RIS效果有限；RIS尺寸增大时RSMA增益更显著；在可靠性要求更严格时RSMA优势更明显

Conclusion: RIS辅助的RSMA技术能有效协同优化延迟和能效，特别适合URLLC等对延迟和可靠性要求严格的应用场景，为未来无线通信系统提供了有前景的解决方案

Abstract: We simultaneously minimize the latency and improve energy efficiency (EE) of the multi-user multiple-input multiple-output (MU-MIMO) rate splitting multiple access (RSMA) downlink, aided by a reconfigurable intelligent surface (RIS). Our results show that RSMA improves the EE and may reduce the delay to 13\% of that of spatial division multiple access (SDMA). Moreover, RIS and RSMA support each other synergistically, while an RIS operating without RSMA provides limited benefits in terms of latency and cannot effectively mitigate interference. {Furthermore, increasing the RIS size amplifies the gains of RSMA more significantly than those of SDMA, without altering the fundamental EE-latency trade-offs.} Results also show that latency increases with more stringent reliability requirements, and RSMA yields more significant gains under such conditions, making it eminently suitable for energy-efficient ultra-reliable low-latency communication (URLLC) scenarios.

</details>


### [27] [Fast Volume Alignment by Frequency-Marched Newton](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.15285)
*Fabian Kruse,Valentin Debarnot,Vinith Kishore,Ivan Dokmanić*

Main category: eess.SP

TL;DR: 提出了一种快速准确的3D对齐方法，通过连续优化和频域处理显著提升配准效率，在保持精度的同时大幅减少计算时间。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统匹配滤波方法需要在大规模离散变换空间评估互相关，计算效率低下。需要开发一种既能保持高精度又能显著提升计算效率的3D对齐方法。

Method: 采用带限Wigner-D展开旋转相关性，实现快速评估和闭式梯度/海森矩阵计算。结合粗粒度SO(3)搜索提供初始候选，通过迭代频率推进和牛顿步骤进行精炼，在交替方案中通过FFT更新平移。

Result: 在合成旋转估计基准测试中达到亚度精度，相比穷举SO(3)搜索显著减少运行时间。集成到RELION5子断层图平均流程中，保持基线重建质量，达到奈奎斯特极限的局部分辨率，同时将姿态精炼时间减少一个数量级以上。

Conclusion: 该方法通过连续优化和频域处理实现了高效准确的3D对齐，在保持重建质量的同时大幅提升了计算效率，为大规模3D数据处理提供了实用解决方案。

Abstract: We develop a fast and accurate method for 3D alignment, recovering the rotation and translation that best align a reference volume with a noisy observation. Classical matched filtering evaluates cross-correlation over a large discretized transformation space; we show that high-precision alignment can be achieved far more efficiently by treating pose estimation as a continuous optimization problem. Our starting point is a band-limited Wigner-$D$ expansion of the rotational correlation, which enables rapid evaluation and efficient closed-form gradients and Hessians. Combined with analytical control of the complexity of trigonometric-polynomial landscapes, this makes second-order optimization practical in a setting where it is often avoided due to nonconvexity and noise sensitivity. We show that Newton-type refinement is stable and effective when initialized at low angular bandwidth: a coarse low-resolution $\mathrm{SO}(3)$ search provides robust candidates, which are then refined by iterative frequency marching and Newton steps, with translations updated via FFT in an alternating scheme. We provide a deterministic convergence guarantee showing that, under verifiable spectral-decay and gap conditions, the frequency-marching scheme returns a near-optimal solution whose suboptimality is controlled by the Newton tolerance. On synthetic rotation-estimation benchmarks, the method attains sub-degree accuracy while substantially reducing runtime relative to exhaustive $\mathrm{SO}(3)$ search. Integrated into the subtomogram-averaging pipeline of RELION5, it matches the baseline reconstruction quality, reaching local resolution at the Nyquist limit, while reducing pose-refinement time by more than an order of magnitude.

</details>


### [28] [On the CRLB for Blind Receiver I/Q Imbalance Estimation in OFDM Systems: Efficient Computation and Closed-Form Bounds](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.15310)
*Moritz Tockner,Oliver Lang,Andreas Meingassner-Lang,Mario Huemer*

Main category: eess.SP

TL;DR: 本文扩展了先前会议论文，为盲估计频率无关接收机I/Q不平衡推导了Cramér-Rao下界，提出了计算效率更高的方法，并提供了简化闭式近似，从而改进了现有估计方法的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 直接变频接收机架构对I/Q路径的幅度和相位失配敏感，会引入不需要的镜像分量，降低链路性能。虽然已有数据辅助估计的CRLB，但盲估计情况下的CRLB推导更具挑战性，因为接收数据是随机的且通常在频域非高斯分布。

Method: 扩展先前会议论文，为盲估计频率无关接收机I/Q不平衡推导CRLB。提出计算效率更高的方法，将复杂度从样本数的立方降低到FFT大小的线性复杂度，并提供简化闭式近似。基于近似结果，提出预估计滤波修改以改进现有估计方法的性能。

Result: 成功推导了盲I/Q不平衡估计的CRLB，开发了计算效率高的计算方法，并提供了简化闭式近似。该近似揭示了现有估计方法的分配依赖性能，启发了预估计滤波修改，在特定场景下显著提高了估计性能。

Conclusion: 本文为盲I/Q不平衡估计提供了理论性能界限，提出的高效计算方法和闭式近似不仅降低了计算复杂度，还提供了对现有估计方法性能的新见解，并通过预估计滤波修改显著提升了实际估计性能。

Abstract: Modern mobile communication receivers are often implemented with a direct-conversion architecture, which features a number of advantages over competing designs. A notable limitation of direct-conversion architectures, however, is their sensitivity to amplitude and phase mismatches between the in-phase and quadrature signal paths. Such in-phase and quadrature-phase (I/Q) imbalances introduce undesired image components in the baseband signal, degrading link performance -- most notably by increasing the bit-error ratio. Considerable research effort has therefore been devoted to digital techniques for estimating and mitigating these impairments. Existing approaches generally fall into two categories: data-aided methods that exploit known pilots, preambles, or training sequences, and blind techniques that operate without such prior information. For data-aided estimation, Cramér-Rao lower bounds (CRLBs) have been established in the literature. In contrast, the derivation of a CRLB for the blind I/Q-imbalance estimation case is considerably more challenging, since the received data is random and typically non-Gaussian in the frequency domain. This work extends our earlier conference contribution, which introduced a CRLB derivation for the blind estimation of frequency-independent (FID) receiver I/Q imbalance using central limit theorem (CLT) arguments. The extensions include a computationally efficient method for calculating the bound, reducing complexity from cubic in the number of samples to linear in the fast-Fourier transform (FFT) size, along with a simplified closed-form approximation. This approximation provides new insights into the allocation dependent performances of existing estimation methods, motivating a pre-estimation filtering modification that drastically improves their estimation performance in certain scenarios.

</details>


### [29] [Near-field Boundary Distance in mmWave and THz Communications with Misaligned Antenna Arrays](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.15311)
*Peng Zhang,Vitaly Petrov,Emil Björnson*

Main category: eess.SP

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个通用数学模型来表征毫米波和太赫兹通信系统中天线阵列存在旋转和不对齐情况下的辐射近场距离边界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 毫米波和太赫兹通信利用大频段实现超高数据速率，但其短波长导致扩展的辐射近场区域，传统远场假设失效。现有研究要么不考虑两端天线阵列，要么假设完美对齐，而实际部署中两端都需要大规模天线阵列且无法保证完美对齐，特别是在节点移动时。

Method: 提出了一个通用数学框架来表征定向毫米波和太赫兹通信系统中各种实际阵列旋转和不对齐情况下的辐射近场距离。基于该框架，推导了紧凑的闭式表达式，涵盖阵列到阵列和阵列到点设置，包括线性和平面阵列配置。

Result: 数值研究表明，天线不对齐的存在可能显著调整毫米波和太赫兹通信系统中近场区域的边界。

Conclusion: 本文提供了一个分析工具，用于准确评估实际部署中毫米波和太赫兹通信系统的近场效应，特别是在天线阵列存在旋转和不对齐的情况下，这对系统设计和性能评估至关重要。

Abstract: Wireless communications in the millimeter wave (mmWave) and terahertz (THz) spectrum allow harnessing large frequency bands, thus achieving ultra-high data rates. However, the inherently short wavelengths of mmWave and THz signals lead to an extended radiative near-field region, where certain canonical far-field assumptions fail. Most prior works aimed to characterize this radiative near-field region either do not consider antenna arrays on both communicating nodes or, if they do, assume perfect alignment between the arrays. However, such assumptions break down in many realistic deployments, where both sides must employ large-scale mmWave/THz antenna arrays to maintain the desired communication range, while perfect antenna alignment cannot be guaranteed particularly under nodes mobility. In this work, a generalized mathematical framework is presented to characterize the radiative near-field distance in directional mmWave and THz communication systems under various realistic array rotations and misalignments. With the use of the developed framework, compact closed-form expressions are derived for the near-field boundary distance in a wide range of antenna configurations, including array-to-array and array-to-point setups, considering both linear and planar arrays. Our numerical study reveals that the presence of antenna misalignment may significantly adjust the boundaries of the near-field region in mmWave and THz communication systems.

</details>

### [30] [Hybrid Energy-Aware Reward Shaping: A Unified Lightweight Physics-Guided Methodology for Policy Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.11600)
*Qijun Liao,Jue Yang,Yiting Kang,Xinxin Zhao,Yong Zhang,Mingan Zhao*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: H-EARS：结合基于势能的奖励塑造与能量感知动作正则化的混合方法，通过功能分解平衡任务特定和能量势能，实现线性复杂度，提升强化学习的收敛性、稳定性和能量效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 深度强化学习在连续控制中表现出色但需要大量探索，而基于物理的模型需要完整方程且具有立方复杂度。需要一种方法既能利用轻量级物理先验增强模型无关RL，又不需要完整系统模型。

Method: 提出混合能量感知奖励塑造（H-EARS），统一基于势能的奖励塑造与能量感知动作正则化。通过功能分解约束动作幅度，平衡任务特定和能量势能，仅捕获主导能量成分而不需要完整动力学，实现O(n)线性复杂度。

Result: 建立了理论基础：功能独立性、基于能量的收敛加速、函数逼近下的收敛保证、近似势能误差边界。实验显示改进的收敛性、稳定性和能量效率。车辆仿真验证了在极端条件下安全关键领域的适用性。

Conclusion: 集成轻量级物理先验可以增强模型无关强化学习，无需完整系统模型，实现从实验室研究到工业应用的转移。H-EARS为连续控制问题提供了高效、稳定的解决方案。

Abstract: Deep reinforcement learning excels in continuous control but often requires extensive exploration, while physics-based models demand complete equations and suffer cubic complexity. This study proposes Hybrid Energy-Aware Reward Shaping (H-EARS), unifying potential-based reward shaping with energy-aware action regularization. H-EARS constrains action magnitude while balancing task-specific and energy-based potentials via functional decomposition, achieving linear complexity O(n) by capturing dominant energy components without full dynamics. We establish a theoretical foundation including: (1) functional independence for separate task/energy optimization; (2) energy-based convergence acceleration; (3) convergence guarantees under function approximation; and (4) approximate potential error bounds. Lyapunov stability connections are analyzed as heuristic guides. Experiments across baselines show improved convergence, stability, and energy efficiency. Vehicle simulations validate applicability in safety-critical domains under extreme conditions. Results confirm that integrating lightweight physics priors enhances model-free RL without complete system models, enabling transfer from lab research to industrial applications.

</details>


### [31] [Translational Gaps in Graph Transformers for Longitudinal EHR Prediction: A Critical Appraisal of GT-BEHRT](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.13231)
*Krish Tadigotla*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文对GT-BEHRT图-Transformer架构进行批判性评估，发现其在心衰预测上表现优异（AUROC 94.37），但存在校准分析缺失、公平性评估不完整、对队列选择敏感等局限性，需要更严格的评估才能支持临床决策。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统Transformer模型将临床就诊视为无序代码集合，限制了捕捉就诊内有意义关系的能力。图-Transformer方法旨在解决这一限制，同时保持学习长期时间模式的能力。本文旨在批判性评估GT-BEHRT架构是否真正带来性能提升，以及其评估方法是否支持稳健性和临床相关性的主张。

Method: 对GT-BEHRT图-Transformer架构进行批判性评估，分析其在七个维度上的表现：表示设计、预训练策略、队列构建透明度、超越区分度的评估、公平性评估、可重复性和部署可行性。评估基于MIMIC-IV重症监护结果和All of Us研究计划中的心衰预测。

Result: GT-BEHRT在心衰预测（365天内）上表现出强大的区分能力：AUROC 94.37 ± 0.20，AUPRC 73.96 ± 0.83，F1 64.70 ± 0.85。但研究发现存在重要缺陷：缺乏校准分析、公平性评估不完整、对队列选择敏感、跨表型和预测时间范围的分析有限、实际部署考虑讨论不足。

Conclusion: GT-BEHRT代表了电子健康记录表示学习的有意义的架构进步，但在这些模型能够可靠地支持临床决策之前，需要更严格的评估，重点关注校准、公平性和部署可行性。

Abstract: Transformer-based models have improved predictive modeling on longitudinal electronic health records through large-scale self-supervised pretraining. However, most EHR transformer architectures treat each clinical encounter as an unordered collection of codes, which limits their ability to capture meaningful relationships within a visit. Graph-transformer approaches aim to address this limitation by modeling visit-level structure while retaining the ability to learn long-term temporal patterns. This paper provides a critical review of GT-BEHRT, a graph-transformer architecture evaluated on MIMIC-IV intensive care outcomes and heart failure prediction in the All of Us Research Program. We examine whether the reported performance gains reflect genuine architectural benefits and whether the evaluation methodology supports claims of robustness and clinical relevance. We analyze GT-BEHRT across seven dimensions relevant to modern machine learning systems, including representation design, pretraining strategy, cohort construction transparency, evaluation beyond discrimination, fairness assessment, reproducibility, and deployment feasibility. GT-BEHRT reports strong discrimination for heart failure prediction within 365 days, with AUROC 94.37 +/- 0.20, AUPRC 73.96 +/- 0.83, and F1 64.70 +/- 0.85. Despite these results, we identify several important gaps, including the lack of calibration analysis, incomplete fairness evaluation, sensitivity to cohort selection, limited analysis across phenotypes and prediction horizons, and limited discussion of practical deployment considerations. Overall, GT-BEHRT represents a meaningful architectural advance in EHR representation learning, but more rigorous evaluation focused on calibration, fairness, and deployment is needed before such models can reliably support clinical decision-making.

</details>


### [32] [AI-Driven Predictive Maintenance with Real-Time Contextual Data Fusion for Connected Vehicles: A Multi-Dataset Evaluation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.13343)
*Kushal Khemani,Anjum Nazir Qureshi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出一个基于V2X增强的车辆预测性维护框架，整合车载传感器与外部环境信号，在车辆边缘进行推理，实验显示V2X特征能提升2.6点F1分数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有车辆预测性维护系统主要依赖内部诊断信号，且多在确定性合成数据上验证，限制了报告指标的可靠性。需要整合外部环境信号并通过V2X通信获取，以提高预测准确性。

Method: 提出仿真验证的概念验证框架，整合车载传感器流与通过V2X通信和第三方API获取的外部环境信号（道路质量、天气、交通密度、驾驶行为），在车辆边缘进行推理。使用LightGBM模型，进行特征组消融研究、噪声敏感性分析和SHAP分析。

Result: V2X环境特征贡献2.6点F1增益；在AI4I 2020真实工业故障数据集上，LightGBM达到0.973的AUC-ROC；噪声敏感性分析显示在低噪声下宏F1保持在0.88以上；边缘推理将延迟从3.5秒降至1.0秒以下。

Conclusion: V2X增强的预测性维护框架能有效整合外部环境信号提升预测性能，边缘推理显著降低延迟。下一步需要进行现场车辆验证。

Abstract: Most vehicle predictive maintenance systems rely exclusively on internal diagnostic signals and are validated on deterministic synthetic data, limiting the credibility of reported metrics. This paper presents a simulation-validated proof-of-concept framework for V2X-augmented predictive maintenance, integrating on-board sensor streams with external contextual signals -- road quality, weather, traffic density, and driver behaviour -- acquired via V2X communication and third-party APIs, with inference at the vehicle edge. Field validation on instrumented vehicles is identified as the required next step. Three experiments address common shortcomings of prior work. A feature group ablation study shows that V2X contextual features contribute a 2.6-point F1 gain, with full context removal reducing macro F1 from 0.855 to 0.807. On the AI4I 2020 real-world industrial failure dataset (10,000 samples, five failure modes), LightGBM achieves AUC-ROC of 0.973 under 5-fold stratified CV with SMOTE confined to training folds. A noise sensitivity analysis shows macro F1 remains above 0.88 under low noise and degrades to 0.74 under very high noise. SHAP analysis confirms that V2X and engineered interaction features rank among the top 15 predictors. Edge inference is estimated to reduce latency from 3.5s to under 1.0s versus cloud-only processing.

</details>


### [33] [RFX-Fuse: Breiman and Cutler's Unified ML Engine + Native Explainable Similarity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.13234)
*Chris Kuchar*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: RFX-Fuse实现了Breiman和Cutler原始随机森林的完整愿景，将分类、回归、无监督学习、相似性计算、异常检测、缺失值插补和可视化等功能统一到单个模型对象中，支持GPU/CPU加速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代机器学习流程需要使用5个以上独立工具（如XGBoost、FAISS、SHAP等），而Breiman和Cutler的原始随机森林设计是一个统一的ML引擎，包含多种功能，但现代库从未完全实现这些功能。

Method: RFX-Fuse基于单组树结构，一次训练即可支持多种功能。主要创新包括：(1) 邻近重要性——可解释的相似性度量，既能衡量样本相似度，又能解释相似原因；(2) 数据集特定的插补验证——无需真实标签即可评估插补方法的质量。

Result: RFX-Fuse提供了一个1到2个模型对象的替代方案，取代了传统需要多个独立工具的复杂流程，实现了随机森林的完整功能集。

Conclusion: RFX-Fuse实现了Breiman和Cutler的原始愿景，将随机森林作为统一的机器学习引擎，简化了现代ML流程，减少了工具依赖，提高了效率。

Abstract: Breiman and Cutler's original Random Forest was designed as a unified ML engine -- not merely an ensemble predictor. Their implementation included classification, regression, unsupervised learning, proximity-based similarity, outlier
  detection, missing value imputation, and visualization -- capabilities that modern libraries like scikit-learn never implemented. RFX-Fuse (Random Forests X [X=compression] -- Forest Unified Learning and Similarity Engine) delivers
  Breiman and Cutler's complete vision with native GPU/CPU support. Modern ML pipelines require 5+ separate tools -- XGBoost for prediction, FAISS for similarity, SHAP for explanations, Isolation Forest for outliers, custom code for
  importance. RFX-Fuse provides a 1 to 2 model object alternative -- a single set of trees grown once. Novel Contributions: (1) Proximity Importance -- native explainable similarity: proximity measures that samples are similar;
  proximity importance explains why. (2) Dataset-specific imputation validation for general tabular data -- ranking imputation methods by how real the imputed data looks, without ground truth labels.

</details>


### [34] [Continual Fine-Tuning with Provably Accurate and Parameter-Free Task Retrieval](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.13235)
*Hang Thi-Thuy Le,Long Minh Bui,Minh Hoang,Trong Nghia Hoang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出一种结合输入适应和参数适应优势的持续微调方法，通过参数自由检索实现测试时自适应表示使用


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有持续微调方法存在两难：输入适应方法需要学习检索函数但容易遗忘，参数适应方法使用固定输入嵌入但牺牲表示适应性。需要结合两者优势。

Method: 提出参数适应方法，包含两个关键组件：(1)自适应模块组合策略学习任务特定更新以保留和补充先验知识；(2)基于聚类的检索机制捕获每个任务的独特表示特征，实现测试时自适应表示使用。

Result: 广泛实验表明，这些组件协同工作，在任务语义大幅变化时显著提升检索和预测性能。

Conclusion: 通过理论分析和实验验证，证明了良好组织的聚类结构能够实现可靠检索，提出的方法成功结合了输入适应和参数适应的优势。

Abstract: Continual fine-tuning aims to adapt a pre-trained backbone to new tasks sequentially while preserving performance on earlier tasks whose data are no longer available. Existing approaches fall into two categories which include input- and parameter-adaptation. Input-adaptation methods rely on retrieving the most relevant prompts at test time, but require continuously learning a retrieval function that is prone to forgetting. Parameter-adaptation methods instead use a fixed input embedding function to enable retrieval-free prediction and avoid forgetting, but sacrifice representation adaptability. To combine their best strengths, we propose a new parameter-adaptation method that enables adaptive use of input embeddings during test time with parameter-free retrieval. We derive task-retrieval error bounds for a clustering-based, parameter-free paradigm, providing theoretical guarantees that link low retrieval error to structural properties of task-specific representation clusters, revealing a fresh insight into how well-organized clustering structure will enable reliable retrieval. Motivated by this insight, our method is designed with two key components: (i) an adaptive module composition strategy that learns informative task-specific updates to preserve and complement prior knowledge, and (ii) a clustering-based retrieval mechanism that captures distinct representation signatures for each task, enabling adaptive representation use at test time. Extensive experiments show that these components work synergistically to improve retrieval and predictive performance under large shifts in task semantics.

</details>


### [35] [High-Probability Bounds for SGD under the Polyak-Lojasiewicz Condition with Markovian Noise](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.14514)
*Avik Kar,Siddharth Chandak,Rahul Singh,Eric Moulines,Shalabh Bhatnagar,Nicholas Bambos*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 首次提出在PL条件下SGD的均匀时间高概率界，适用于包含马尔可夫和鞅差噪声的梯度噪声，扩展了有限时间保证的范围


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: PL条件在机器学习和深度学习模型中普遍存在，而马尔可夫噪声在分布式优化和在线系统识别中自然出现，现有分析缺乏对此类噪声的统一时间保证

Method: 使用泊松方程处理马尔可夫噪声，采用概率归纳论证处理目标函数缺乏几乎确定界的问题，允许噪声幅度随函数值增长

Result: 建立了均匀时间高概率界，证明了期望次优性的匹配1/k衰减率，并将框架应用于三个实际问题：基于令牌的分布式线性回归、隐私放大的子采样监督学习、在线系统识别

Conclusion: 该框架显著扩展了SGD有限时间保证的适用范围，能够处理包含马尔可夫和鞅差噪声的PL条件优化问题，为实际应用提供了理论保证

Abstract: We present the first uniform-in-time high-probability bound for SGD under the PL condition, where the gradient noise contains both Markovian and martingale difference components. This significantly broadens the scope of finite-time guarantees, as the PL condition arises in many machine learning and deep learning models while Markovian noise naturally arises in decentralized optimization and online system identification problems. We further allow the magnitude of noise to grow with the function value, enabling the analysis of many practical sampling strategies. In addition to the high-probability guarantee, we establish a matching $1/k$ decay rate for the expected suboptimality. Our proof technique relies on the Poisson equation to handle the Markovian noise and a probabilistic induction argument to address the lack of almost-sure bounds on the objective. Finally, we demonstrate the applicability of our framework by analyzing three practical optimization problems: token-based decentralized linear regression, supervised learning with subsampling for privacy amplification, and online system identification.

</details>


### [36] [Introducing Feature-Based Trajectory Clustering, a clustering algorithm for longitudinal data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.13254)
*Marie-Pierre Sylvestre,Laurence Boulanger*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出一种新的纵向数据聚类算法，通过特征提取将个体映射到欧氏空间，再应用谱聚类发现具有相似时间演化模式的个体群组。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 纵向数据中，不同个体的时间依赖性变量演化方式各异，但可能存在许多个体共享的共性特征模式。需要一种方法能够识别出具有相似时间演化特征的个体群组。

Method: 采用两步法：第一步通过特定数学公式提取个体时间演化特征，将每个个体映射到欧氏空间中的点；第二步对得到的点云应用谱聚类算法进行聚类分析。

Result: 提出了一种新的纵向数据聚类框架，能够有效识别具有相似时间演化特征的个体群组，为纵向数据分析提供了新的工具。

Conclusion: 该方法通过特征提取和谱聚类相结合，能够有效发现纵向数据中共享特定时间演化模式的个体群组，为纵向数据分析提供了实用的聚类解决方案。

Abstract: We present a new algorithm for clustering longitudinal data. Data of this type can be conceptualized as consisting of individuals and, for each such individual, observations of a time-dependent variable made at various times. Generically, the specific way in which this variable evolves with time is different from one individual to the next. However, there may also be commonalities; specific characteristic features of the time evolution shared by many individuals. The purpose of the method we put forward is to find clusters of individual whose underlying time-dependent variables share such characteristic features. This is done in two steps. The first step identifies each individual to a point in Euclidean space whose coordinates are determined by specific mathematical formulae meant to capture a variety of characteristic features. The second step finds the clusters by applying the Spectral Clustering algorithm to the resulting point cloud.

</details>


### [37] [Mechanistic Foundations of Goal-Directed Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.15248)
*Alma Lago*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该研究将机制可解释性框架扩展到感觉运动认知发展领域，以婴儿运动学习为模型系统，揭示了反应性和前瞻性控制策略如何在学习过程中形成和竞争。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机制可解释性在分析transformer电路方面取得了成功，但主要局限于序列预测架构，缺乏对具身控制系统的类似机制解释。本研究旨在将这一框架扩展到感觉运动认知发展领域。

Method: 使用婴儿运动学习作为模型系统，分析基础归纳偏差如何产生因果控制电路，研究学习门控机制如何收敛到理论上的不确定性阈值。识别上下文窗口k作为电路形成的关键参数。

Result: 发现仲裁门存在清晰的相变，其承诺行为可由闭式指数移动平均替代模型描述。上下文窗口k是关键参数：k≤4时仲裁机制无法形成；k≥8时，门置信度按log k渐近缩放。二维相图揭示了任务需求相关的路径仲裁。

Conclusion: 该研究为反应性和前瞻性控制策略在学习过程中如何出现和竞争提供了机制解释，同时为认知发展的机制解释提供了更精确的框架，并为设计可解释的具身智能体提供了原则性指导。

Abstract: Mechanistic interpretability has transformed the analysis of transformer circuits by decomposing model behavior into competing algorithms, identifying phase transitions during training, and deriving closed-form predictions for when and why strategies shift. However, this program has remained largely confined to sequence-prediction architectures, leaving embodied control systems without comparable mechanistic accounts. Here we extend this framework to sensorimotor-cognitive development, using infant motor learning as a model system. We show that foundational inductive biases give rise to causal control circuits, with learned gating mechanisms converging toward theoretically motivated uncertainty thresholds. The resulting dynamics reveal a clean phase transition in the arbitration gate whose commitment behavior is well described by a closed-form exponential moving-average surrogate. We identify context window k as the critical parameter governing circuit formation: below a minimum threshold (k$\leq$4) the arbitration mechanism cannot form; above it (k$\geq$8), gate confidence scales asymptotically as log k. A two-dimensional phase diagram further reveals task-demand-dependent route arbitration consistent with the prediction that prospective execution becomes advantageous only when prediction error remains within the task tolerance window. Together, these results provide a mechanistic account of how reactive and prospective control strategies emerge and compete during learning. More broadly, this work sharpens mechanistic accounts of cognitive development and provides principled guidance for the design of interpretable embodied agents.

</details>
