As proof-of-concept, we use samples of randomly perturbed orthogonal vectors of moderate dimension (2 to 20), with a corresponding number of classes equal to the vector dimension, and show how our approach can both significantly accelerate the training process and improve the classification accuracy compared to a standard softmax classifier which operates on the original vectors, as well as a softmax classifier which only lifts the vectors to a higher-dimensional space without evolving them. We also provide an explanation for the improved performance of the chaos-enhanced classifier.

</details>


### [264] [xplainfi: Feature Importance and Statistical Inference for Machine Learning in R](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.15306)
*Lukas Burk,Fiona Katharina Ewald,Giuseppe Casalicchio,Marvin N. Wright,Bernd Bischl*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: xplainfi是一个基于mlr3生态系统的R包，提供全局、基于损失的特征重要性方法，填补了R语言中条件重要性方法和统计推断的空白。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: R语言中已有多种特征重要性方法，但在条件重要性方法和相关统计推断程序方面存在显著空白，需要更全面的工具包来支持特征重要性分析和模型解释。

Method: 实现多种特征重要性方法：排列特征重要性、条件特征重要性、相对特征重要性、留一协变量法及其泛化，以及边际和条件Shapley加性全局重要性方法。提供基于高斯分布、对抗随机森林、条件推断树和knockoff采样器的模块化条件采样架构。

Result: xplainfi在多种模拟设置和学习器类型中产生与现有实现一致的重要性分数，同时提供具有竞争力的运行时性能。该包已在CRAN上可用。

Conclusion: xplainfi为研究人员和实践者提供了在R中进行特征重要性分析和模型解释的全面工具包，填补了现有方法的空白。

Abstract: We introduce xplainfi, an R package built on top of the mlr3 ecosystem for global, loss-based feature importance methods for machine learning models. Various feature importance methods exist in R, but significant gaps remain, particularly regarding conditional importance methods and associated statistical inference procedures. The package implements permutation feature importance, conditional feature importance, relative feature importance, leave-one-covariate-out, and generalizations thereof, and both marginal and conditional Shapley additive global importance methods. It provides a modular conditional sampling architecture based on Gaussian distributions, adversarial random forests, conditional inference trees, and knockoff-based samplers, which enable conditional importance analysis for continuous and mixed data. Statistical inference is available through multiple approaches, including variance-corrected confidence intervals and the conditional predictive impact framework. We demonstrate that xplainfi produces importance scores consistent with existing implementations across multiple simulation settings and learner types, while offering competitive runtime performance. The package is available on CRAN and provides researchers and practitioners with a comprehensive toolkit for feature importance analysis and model interpretation in R.

</details>


### [265] [A Kolmogorov-Arnold Surrogate Model for Chemical Equilibria: Application to Solid Solutions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.15307)
*Leonardo Boledi,Dirk Bosbach,Jenna Poonoosamy*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该研究首次将Kolmogorov-Arnold网络应用于地球化学代理模型，在水泥系统和核废料地质处置的放射性核素固溶体溶解度预测中，相比传统多层感知机显著提升了精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 地球化学求解器的计算成本高昂，在需要执行数十亿次化学计算的反应输运模拟中尤为突出。现有研究探索了多种机器学习方法构建数据驱动的代理模型，但需要寻找更有效的架构来减少计算时间。

Method: 采用新型Kolmogorov-Arnold网络架构，用可学习的样条基函数替代传统的固定激活函数。首先在现有水泥系统基准上训练代理模型，然后应用于核废料地质处置场景，预测含放射性核素固体的溶解度，考虑了从简单机械混合物到非理想固溶体的不同热力学复杂度。

Result: 在水泥基准测试中，Kolmogorov-Arnold架构在绝对和相对误差指标上均优于多层感知机，分别减少了62%和59%。在二元和三元镭固溶体模型中，Kolmogorov-Arnold网络的中位预测误差保持在约1×10⁻³。

Conclusion: 这是首次使用数据驱动代理模型研究放射性核素共沉淀的研究，Kolmogorov-Arnold网络在减少可训练参数的同时实现了更高精度，为加速反应输运模拟和优化深地质废物储存库安全评估迈出了重要一步。

Abstract: The computational cost of geochemical solvers is a challenging matter. For reactive transport simulations, where chemical calculations are performed up to billions of times, it is crucial to reduce the total computational time. Existing publications have explored various machine-learning approaches to determine the most effective data-driven surrogate model. In particular, multilayer perceptrons are widely employed due to their ability to recognize nonlinear relationships. In this work, we focus on the recent Kolmogorov-Arnold networks, where learnable spline-based functions replace classical fixed activation functions. This architecture has achieved higher accuracy with fewer trainable parameters and has become increasingly popular for solving partial differential equations. First, we train a surrogate model based on an existing cement system benchmark. Then, we move to an application case for the geological disposal of nuclear waste, i.e., the determination of radionuclide-bearing solids solubilities. To the best of our knowledge, this work is the first to investigate co-precipitation with radionuclide incorporation using data-driven surrogate models, considering increasing levels of thermodynamic complexity from simple mechanical mixtures to non-ideal solid solutions of binary (Ba,Ra)SO$_4$ and ternary (Sr,Ba,Ra)SO$_4$ systems. On the cement benchmark, we demonstrate that the Kolmogorov-Arnold architecture outperforms multilayer perceptrons in both absolute and relative error metrics, reducing them by 62% and 59%, respectively. On the binary and ternary radium solid solution models, Kolmogorov-Arnold networks maintain median prediction errors near $1\times10^{-3}$. This is the first step toward employing surrogate models to speed up reactive transport simulations and optimize the safety assessment of deep geological waste repositories.

</details>


### [266] [CASHomon Sets: Efficient Rashomon Sets Across Multiple Model Classes and their Hyperparameters](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.15321)
*Fiona Katharina Ewald,Martin Binder,Matthias Feurer,Bernd Bischl,Giuseppe Casalicchio*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出CASHomon集合概念，将Rashomon集合扩展到算法选择和超参数优化(CASH)场景，开发TruVaRImp算法高效识别这些集合，挑战单一模型类解释数据的传统做法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前Rashomon集合的高效构建方法仅适用于少数模型类，而实际机器学习通常需要搜索多个模型类，且最佳模型类事先未知。因此需要研究CASH场景下的Rashomon集合，以支持跨模型类的模型选择和解释。

Method: 提出CASHomon集合概念，将Rashomon集合扩展到CASH设置。开发TruVaRImp算法，这是一种基于模型的主动学习算法，用于具有隐式阈值的水平集估计，并提供收敛保证。

Result: 在合成和真实数据集上，TruVaRImp能可靠识别CASHomon集合成员，性能匹配或优于朴素采样、贝叶斯优化、经典和隐式水平集估计方法及其他基线。分析显示预测多样性和特征重要性在不同模型类间存在显著差异。

Conclusion: 通过单一模型类解释数据的常见做法值得质疑。CASHomon集合和TruVaRImp算法为跨模型类的模型选择和解释提供了有效工具，支持选择符合领域知识、隐藏约束或用户偏好的模型。

Abstract: Rashomon sets are model sets within one model class that perform nearly as well as a reference model from the same model class. They reveal the existence of alternative well-performing models, which may support different interpretations. This enables selecting models that match domain knowledge, hidden constraints, or user preferences. However, efficient construction methods currently exist for only a few model classes. Applied machine learning usually searches many model classes, and the best class is unknown beforehand. We therefore study Rashomon sets in the combined algorithm selection and hyperparameter optimization (CASH) setting and call them CASHomon sets. We propose TruVaRImp, a model-based active learning algorithm for level set estimation with an implicit threshold, and provide convergence guarantees. On synthetic and real-world datasets, TruVaRImp reliably identifies CASHomon sets members and matches or outperforms naive sampling, Bayesian optimization, classical and implicit level set estimation methods, and other baselines. Our analyses of predictive multiplicity and feature-importance variability across model classes question the common practice of interpreting data through a single model class.

</details>


### [267] [Data Augmentation via Causal-Residual Bootstrapping](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.15335)
*Mateusz Gajewski,Sophia Xiao,Bijan Mazaheri*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出一种基于独立机制原理的数据增强方法，通过置换边际概率分布模型的残差来整合因果知识，超越马尔可夫等价类限制


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有数据增强方法主要整合领域知识（如图像变换），但因果知识整合有限。Teshima和Sugiyama的工作探索了因果知识整合，但仅限于条件独立性等价类。需要一种方法能在加性噪声设置中整合超越马尔可夫等价类的因果信息

Method: 基于独立机制原理，构建在边际概率分布模型上，通过置换模型残差来增强数据。该方法特别适用于加性噪声设置，能够整合超越马尔可夫等价类的因果知识

Result: 基于增强数据构建的预测模型展现出更高的准确性。在线性高斯设置中提供了理论支持，验证了方法的有效性

Conclusion: 提出了一种新的数据增强方法，能够有效整合超越马尔可夫等价类的因果知识，在加性噪声设置中提升预测模型性能，为因果知识整合提供了新思路

Abstract: Data augmentation integrates domain knowledge into a dataset by making domain-informed modifications to existing data points. For example, image data can be augmented by duplicating images in different tints or orientations, thereby incorporating the knowledge that images may vary in these dimensions. Recent work by Teshima and Sugiyama has explored the integration of causal knowledge (e.g, A causes B causes C) up to conditional independence equivalence. We suggest a related approach for settings with additive noise that can incorporate information beyond a Markov equivalence class. The approach, built on the principle of independent mechanisms, permutes the residuals of models built on marginal probability distributions. Predictive models built on our augmented data demonstrate improved accuracy, for which we provide theoretical backing in linear Gaussian settings.

</details>


### [268] [Conditional Rectified Flow-based End-to-End Rapid Seismic Inversion Method](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.15354)
*Haofei Xu,Wei Cheng,Sizhe Li,Jie Xiong*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出基于条件整流流的端到端快速地震反演方法，通过专用地震编码器和分层注入控制策略，在OpenFWI数据集上实现高精度反演，相比扩散方法加速采样，相比InversionNet方法提高精度，并在Marmousi数据上验证零样本泛化能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统地震反演方法计算成本高且依赖初始模型，现有深度生成模型难以平衡采样效率和反演精度，需要开发更高效准确的地震反演方法。

Method: 基于条件整流流(Conditional Rectified Flow)的端到端方法，设计专用地震编码器提取多尺度地震特征，采用分层注入控制策略实现细粒度条件控制。

Result: 在OpenFWI基准数据集上取得优异反演精度，相比扩散方法实现采样加速，相比InversionNet方法获得更高生成精度，在Marmousi真实数据上验证零样本泛化能力，能生成高质量初始速度模型。

Conclusion: 该方法能有效缓解传统全波形反演中的初始模型依赖问题，具有工业实用价值，为地震反演提供了高效准确的解决方案。

Abstract: Seismic inversion is a core problem in geophysical exploration, where traditional methods suffer from high computational costs and are susceptible to initial model dependence. In recent years, deep generative model-based seismic inversion methods have achieved remarkable progress, but existing generative models struggle to balance sampling efficiency and inversion accuracy. This paper proposes an end-to-end fast seismic inversion method based on Conditional Rectified Flow[1], which designs a dedicated seismic encoder to extract multi-scale seismic features and adopts a layer-by-layer injection control strategy to achieve fine-grained conditional control. Experimental results demonstrate that the proposed method achieves excellent inversion accuracy on the OpenFWI[2] benchmark dataset. Compared with Diffusion[3,4] methods, it achieves sampling acceleration; compared with InversionNet[5,6,7] methods, it achieves higher accuracy in generation. Our zero-shot generalization experiments on Marmousi[8,9] real data further verify the practical value of the method. Experimental results show that the proposed method achieves excellent inversion accuracy on the OpenFWI benchmark dataset; compared with Diffusion methods, it achieves sampling acceleration while maintaining higher accuracy than InversionNet methods; experiments based on the Marmousi standard model further verify that this method can generate high-quality initial velocity models in a zero-shot manner, effectively alleviating the initial model dependency problem in traditional Full Waveform Inversion (FWI), and possesses industrial practical value.

</details>


### [269] [FuXiWeather2: Learning accurate atmospheric state estimation for operational global weather forecasting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.15358)
*Xiaoze Xu,Xiuyu Sun,Songling Zhu,Xiaohui Zhong,Yuanqing Huang,Zijian Zhu,Jun Liu,Hao Li*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: FuXiWeather2是一个统一的端到端神经网络框架，结合数据同化和天气预报，直接使用真实观测和再分析数据进行训练，能够纠正再分析产品的系统性偏差，在10天内提供高分辨率全球分析和预报。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统数值天气预报受限于数据同化和数值建模的计算瓶颈。现有机器学习模型主要作为"再分析产品的模拟器"，保留了系统性偏差和操作延迟，需要开发能够直接处理观测数据并纠正再分析误差的端到端框架。

Method: 提出统一的端到端神经网络框架FuXiWeather2，采用递归展开训练方法处理训练与部署时的分布偏移问题，使用原始和模拟观测的混合数据集训练以缓解观测分布不一致的影响，生成0.25°分辨率的全球分析场和10天预报。

Result: 分析场在大多数变量上超越NCEP-GFS，在低对流层和地表变量上优于ERA5和ECMWF-HRES系统。确定性预报在91%的评估指标上超过HRES系统，在台风路径预测方面表现优异，突显其在极端天气事件快速响应中的实用价值。

Conclusion: FuXiWeather2成功实现了端到端的同化和预报框架，能够有效纠正再分析产品的偏差，提供高质量的分析和预报，在极端天气事件响应中具有重要实用价值，相关数据集已公开可用。

Abstract: Numerical weather prediction has long been constrained by the computational bottlenecks inherent in data assimilation and numerical modeling. While machine learning has accelerated forecasting, existing models largely serve as "emulators of reanalysis products," thereby retaining their systematic biases and operational latencies. Here, we present FuXiWeather2, a unified end-to-end neural framework for assimilation and forecasting. We align training objectives directly with a combination of real-world observations and reanalysis data, enabling the framework to effectively rectify inherent errors within reanalysis products. To address the distribution shift between NWP-derived background inputs during training and self-generated backgrounds during deployment, we introduce a recursive unrolling training method to enhance the precision and stability of analysis generation. Furthermore, our model is trained on a hybrid dataset of raw and simulated observations to mitigate the impact of observational distribution inconsistency. FuXiWeather2 generates high-resolution ($0.25^{\circ}$) global analysis fields and 10-day forecasts within minutes. The analysis fields surpass the NCEP-GFS across most variables and demonstrate superior accuracy over both ERA5 and the ECMWF-HRES system in lower-tropospheric and surface variables. These high-quality analysis fields drive deterministic forecasts that exceed the skill of the HRES system in 91\% of evaluated metrics. Additionally, its outstanding performance in typhoon track prediction underscores its practical value for rapid response to extreme weather events. The FuXiWeather2 analysis dataset is available at https://doi.org/10.5281/zenodo.18872728.

</details>


### [270] [Deep learning and the rate of approximation by flows](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.15363)
*Jingpu Cheng,Qianxiao Li,Ting Lin,Zuowei Shen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 深度残差网络的逼近能力与深度关系的研究，将深度学习中的函数逼近问题转化为微分同胚流的最小时间逼近问题，建立了子Finsler流形上的测地距离框架。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究深度残差网络的逼近能力如何依赖于其深度，将深度学习的函数逼近问题置于连续动力系统框架下，探索深度学习中函数逼近机制与线性逼近理论的本质区别。

Method: 将深度残差网络的逼近问题转化为微分同胚流的最小时间逼近问题，建立子Finsler流形上的测地距离框架，通过变分原理刻画局部几何结构。

Result: 证明了最小逼近时间可以表示为微分同胚子Finsler流形上的测地距离，揭示了目标关系的学习效率与其与网络架构选择的兼容性之间的联系。

Conclusion: 深度学习的函数逼近机制（通过复合或动力学）与线性逼近理论有本质区别：线性空间和基于范数的速率估计被流形和测地距离所取代，这为理解深度学习的理论基础提供了新视角。

Abstract: We investigate the dependence of the approximation capacity of deep residual networks on its depth in a continuous dynamical systems setting. This can be formulated as the general problem of quantifying the minimal time-horizon required to approximate a diffeomorphism by flows driven by a given family $\mathcal F$ of vector fields. We show that this minimal time can be identified as a geodesic distance on a sub-Finsler manifold of diffeomorphisms, where the local geometry is characterised by a variational principle involving $\mathcal F$. This connects the learning efficiency of target relationships to their compatibility with the learning architectural choice. Further, the results suggest that the key approximation mechanism in deep learning, namely the approximation of functions by composition or dynamics, differs in a fundamental way from linear approximation theory, where linear spaces and norm-based rate estimates are replaced by manifolds and geodesic distances.

</details>


### [271] [GradCFA: A Hybrid Gradient-Based Counterfactual and Feature Attribution Explanation Algorithm for Local Interpretation of Neural Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.15373)
*Jacob Sanderson,Hua Mao,Wai Lok Woo*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: GradCFA是一个结合反事实解释和特征归因的混合框架，通过优化可行性、合理性和多样性来提升AI可解释性，并扩展到多分类场景。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着AI在医疗、金融等关键领域的应用，可解释AI变得越来越重要。现有的反事实解释和特征归因方法各有局限，特别是在可行性、合理性和多样性方面存在不平衡问题。

Method: 提出GradCFA混合框架，结合反事实解释和特征归因，通过显式优化可行性、合理性和多样性来生成解释，并扩展到多分类场景。

Result: 与Wachter、DiCE、CARE、SHAP等先进方法相比，GradCFA在有效性、邻近性、稀疏性、合理性和多样性方面表现优异，能生成可行、合理且多样的反事实解释。

Conclusion: GradCFA通过结合反事实解释和特征归因，有效提升了AI模型的可解释性，识别有影响力的特征并验证其影响，推动了可解释AI的发展。

Abstract: Explainable Artificial Intelligence (XAI) is increasingly essential as AI systems are deployed in critical fields such as healthcare and finance, offering transparency into AI-driven decisions. Two major XAI paradigms, counterfactual explanations (CFX) and feature attribution (FA), serve distinct roles in model interpretability. This study introduces GradCFA, a hybrid framework combining CFX and FA to improve interpretability by explicitly optimizing feasibility, plausibility, and diversity - key qualities often unbalanced in existing methods. Unlike most CFX research focused on binary classification, GradCFA extends to multi-class scenarios, supporting a wider range of applications. We evaluate GradCFA's validity, proximity, sparsity, plausibility, and diversity against state-of-the-art methods, including Wachter, DiCE, CARE for CFX, and SHAP for FA. Results show GradCFA effectively generates feasible, plausible, and diverse counterfactuals while offering valuable FA insights. By identifying influential features and validating their impact, GradCFA advances AI interpretability. The code for implementation of this work can be found at: https://github.com/jacob-ws/GradCFs .

</details>


### [272] [More Test-Time Compute Can Hurt: Overestimation Bias in LLM Beam Search](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.15377)
*Gal Dalal,Assaf Hallak,Gal Chechik,Yftach Ziser*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文基于极值理论分析发现，beam搜索宽度存在上限，超过该宽度会因评分器噪声导致性能下降，关键因素在于评分器的信噪比。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究主要关注beam宽度选择的推理效率，但未分析更宽的搜索是否可能损害输出质量。本文旨在回答beam搜索何时会因宽度增加而降低性能的问题。

Method: 基于极值理论进行分析，推导出最大有用beam宽度公式：$\hat{k}$随$(Δ/σ)^2$指数增长，其中Δ是正确路径相对于错误路径的质量优势，σ是评分器噪声。通过实验验证，在三个7B参数模型和十个领域上比较困惑度引导和PRM引导的beam搜索。

Result: 困惑度评分（噪声高）的$\hat{k}=1$：任何测试宽度下搜索都无益处；PRM评分（噪声低）的$\hat{k}≥4$，性能提升高达8.9个百分点。同一模型、同一算法，不同评分器导致$\hat{k}$位于beam宽度范围的两端。

Conclusion: 评分器的信噪比是决定beam宽度选择的关键因素，本文提出了实践中选择beam宽度的诊断指标。

Abstract: Wider beam search should improve LLM reasoning, but when should you stop widening? Prior work on beam width selection has focused on inference efficiency \citep{qin2025dsbd, freitag2017beam}, without analyzing whether wider search can \emph{hurt} output quality. We present an analysis, grounded in Extreme Value Theory, that answers this question. Beam selection over noisy scorer outputs introduces a systematic overestimation bias that grows with the candidate pool size, and we derive a maximum useful beam width $\hat{k}$ beyond which search degrades performance. This critical width depends on the signal-to-noise ratio of the scorer: $\hat{k}$ grows exponentially with $(Δ/σ)^2$, where $Δ> 0$ is the quality advantage of correct paths over incorrect ones and $σ$ is the scorer noise. We validate this theory by comparing perplexity-guided and PRM-guided beam search across three 7B-parameter models and ten domains on MR-BEN (5,975 questions). Perplexity scoring, with its high noise, yields $\hat{k} = 1$: search provides no benefit at any width tested. PRM scoring, with lower noise, yields $\hat{k} \geq 4$, with gains of up to 8.9 percentage points. The same model, the same algorithm, but different scorers place $\hat{k}$ at opposite ends of the beam width range. Our analysis identifies the scorer's signal-to-noise ratio as the key quantity governing beam width selection, and we propose diagnostic indicators for choosing the beam width in practice.

</details>


### [273] [Local Urysohn Width: A Topological Complexity Measure for Classification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.15412)
*Xin Li*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文提出了局部Urysohn宽度作为度量空间上分类问题的复杂度度量，与VC维度等传统度量不同，它刻画问题本身的拓扑几何复杂度，并证明了四个主要结果：严格层次定理、拓扑×几何缩放定律、与VC维度的双向分离、以及样本复杂度下界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统复杂度度量如VC维度、fat-shattering维度和Rademacher复杂度主要刻画假设类的丰富程度，但缺乏对分类问题本身拓扑几何复杂度的度量。作者希望提出一种新的复杂度度量，能够直接反映分类问题在拓扑和几何上的内在复杂性。

Method: 引入局部Urysohn宽度作为新的复杂度度量，定义为在边缘安全区域内正确分类所有点所需的最小连通、直径有界的局部专家数量。通过构造具体的分类问题实例（如圆环束），并运用拓扑几何分析方法来证明理论结果。

Result: 1) 严格层次定理：对于任意整数w≥1，存在连通紧致度量空间上的分类问题，其Urysohn宽度恰好为w；2) 拓扑×几何缩放定律：宽度按Ω(w·L/D₀)缩放；3) 与VC维度的双向分离：存在宽度无界而VC维度有界的问题族，反之亦然；4) 样本复杂度下界：宽度为w的问题需要Ω(w log w)个样本。

Conclusion: 局部Urysohn宽度提供了分类问题拓扑几何复杂度的新视角，与VC维度等传统度量形成互补。该度量揭示了输入空间拓扑结构对分类器复杂度的根本影响，并为理解分类问题的内在难度提供了新的理论框架。

Abstract: We introduce \emph{local Urysohn width}, a complexity measure for classification problems on metric spaces. Unlike VC dimension, fat-shattering dimension, and Rademacher complexity, which characterize the richness of hypothesis \emph{classes}, Urysohn width characterizes the topological-geometric complexity of the classification \emph{problem itself}: the minimum number of connected, diameter-bounded local experts needed to correctly classify all points within a margin-safe region. We prove four main results. First, a \textbf{strict hierarchy theorem}: for every integer $w \geq 1$, there exists a classification problem on a \emph{connected} compact metric space (a bouquet of circles with first Betti number $β_1 = w$) whose Urysohn width is exactly~$w$, establishing that topological complexity of the input space forces classifier complexity. Second, a \textbf{topology $\times$ geometry scaling law}: width scales as $Ω(w \cdot L/D_0)$, where $w$ counts independent loops and $L/D_0$ is the ratio of loop circumference to locality scale. Third, a \textbf{two-way separation from VC dimension}: there exist problem families where width grows unboundedly while VC dimension is bounded by a constant, and conversely, families where VC dimension grows unboundedly while width remains~1. Fourth, a \textbf{sample complexity lower bound}: any learner that must correctly classify all points in the safe region of a width-$w$ problem needs $Ω(w \log w)$ samples, independent of VC dimension.

</details>


### [274] [RESQ: A Unified Framework for REliability- and Security Enhancement of Quantized Deep Neural Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.15413)
*Ali Soltan Mohammadi,Samira Nazari,Ali Azarpeyvand,Mahdi Taheri,Milos Krstic,Michael Huebner,Christian Herglotz,Tara Ghasempouri*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出统一的三阶段框架，通过对抗性微调、故障感知微调和轻量级后训练量化调整，在量化DNN中同时提升对抗攻击和硬件故障的鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 深度神经网络在部署时面临对抗性攻击和硬件故障（如比特翻转）的双重威胁。现有方法通常单独处理这两种问题，缺乏统一的解决方案来平衡攻击鲁棒性和故障鲁棒性。

Method: 三阶段框架：1) 对抗性微调阶段：通过使特征表示对小输入扰动不敏感来提升攻击鲁棒性；2) 故障感知微调阶段：在模拟比特翻转故障下进行微调以增强故障鲁棒性；3) 轻量级后训练调整：集成量化以提高效率并进一步降低故障敏感性，同时不损害攻击鲁棒性。

Result: 在ResNet18、VGG16、EfficientNet和Swin-Tiny上，使用CIFAR-10、CIFAR-100和GTSRB数据集进行实验，攻击鲁棒性提升最高达10.35%，故障鲁棒性提升最高达12.47%，同时在量化网络中保持有竞争力的准确率。

Conclusion: 该框架成功实现了量化DNN中攻击鲁棒性和故障鲁棒性的平衡提升。研究发现故障鲁棒性的改进通常能增强对抗攻击的鲁棒性，但反之不一定成立，揭示了两种鲁棒性之间的不对称交互关系。

Abstract: This work proposes a unified three-stage framework that produces a quantized DNN with balanced fault and attack robustness. The first stage improves attack resilience via fine-tuning that desensitizes feature representations to small input perturbations. The second stage reinforces fault resilience through fault-aware fine-tuning under simulated bit-flip faults. Finally, a lightweight post-training adjustment integrates quantization to enhance efficiency and further mitigate fault sensitivity without degrading attack resilience. Experiments on ResNet18, VGG16, EfficientNet, and Swin-Tiny in CIFAR-10, CIFAR-100, and GTSRB show consistent gains of up to 10.35% in attack resilience and 12.47% in fault resilience, while maintaining competitive accuracy in quantized networks. The results also highlight an asymmetric interaction in which improvements in fault resilience generally increase resilience to adversarial attacks, whereas enhanced adversarial resilience does not necessarily lead to higher fault resilience.

</details>


### [275] [Amplification Effects in Test-Time Reinforcement Learning: Safety and Reasoning Vulnerabilities](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.15417)
*Vanshaj Khattar,Md Rafi ur Rashid,Moumita Choudhury,Jing Liu,Toshiaki Koike-Akino,Ming Jin,Ye Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 测试时训练(TTT)方法通过自一致性奖励提升LLM推理能力，但易受有害提示注入攻击，导致安全放大或有害放大，并伴随推理能力下降。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 测试时训练(TTT)方法能提升大语言模型的推理能力，但依赖测试数据使其易受有害提示注入攻击。本文旨在研究TTT方法的安全漏洞，特别是基于自一致性的测试时强化学习(TTRL)方法。

Method: 研究测试时强化学习(TTRL)方法的安全漏洞，该方法通过多数投票作为奖励信号来奖励自一致性。设计"HarmInject"提示进行对抗性攻击，分析有害提示注入对模型行为的影响。

Result: 有害提示注入会放大模型的现有行为：当基础模型相对安全时产生安全放大，当模型易受注入数据影响时产生有害放大。两种情况下都出现推理能力下降（推理税）。TTRL可被利用来强制模型同时回答越狱和推理查询，导致更强的有害放大。

Conclusion: 通过促进自一致性来增强LLM推理的TTT方法可能导致行为放大和推理退化，凸显了开发更安全TTT方法的必要性。

Abstract: Test-time training (TTT) has recently emerged as a promising method to improve the reasoning abilities of large language models (LLMs), in which the model directly learns from test data without access to labels. However, this reliance on test data also makes TTT methods vulnerable to harmful prompt injections. In this paper, we investigate safety vulnerabilities of TTT methods, where we study a representative self-consistency-based test-time learning method: test-time reinforcement learning (TTRL), a recent TTT method that improves LLM reasoning by rewarding self-consistency using majority vote as a reward signal. We show that harmful prompt injection during TTRL amplifies the model's existing behaviors, i.e., safety amplification when the base model is relatively safe, and harmfulness amplification when it is vulnerable to the injected data. In both cases, there is a decline in reasoning ability, which we refer to as the reasoning tax. We also show that TTT methods such as TTRL can be exploited adversarially using specially designed "HarmInject" prompts to force the model to answer jailbreak and reasoning queries together, resulting in stronger harmfulness amplification. Overall, our results highlight that TTT methods that enhance LLM reasoning by promoting self-consistency can lead to amplification behaviors and reasoning degradation, highlighting the need for safer TTT methods.

</details>


### [276] [Physics-informed fine-tuning of foundation models for partial differential equations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.15431)
*Vlad Medvedev,Leon Armbruster,Christopher Straub,Georg Kruse,Andreas Rosskopf*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出基于物理约束的微调框架，用于适应PDE基础模型到下游任务，在数据稀缺时保持物理一致性


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: PDE基础模型适应新任务面临挑战：任务特定数据有限、分布偏移。虽然微调在NLP中成功，但PDE基础模型的最佳实践尚未充分探索。物理信息训练已广泛用于PDE求解器，但尚未系统研究其在微调数据驱动基础模型中的潜力

Method: 引入物理信息微调框架，将物理约束（PDE残差和边界条件）直接纳入微调目标，适应预训练的PDE基础模型。还提出混合微调策略，结合数据驱动和物理约束方法

Result: 物理信息微调在未见PDE类别的下游任务中达到竞争性精度，无需PDE解进行训练。混合微调策略在仅有最小训练数据时，对分布外场景表现出更优的泛化能力

Conclusion: 物理信息微调为科学机器学习中适应基础模型提供了可扩展、数据高效且物理可解释的范式

Abstract: Foundation models for partial differential equations (PDEs) have emerged as powerful surrogates pre-trained on diverse physical systems, but adapting them to new downstream tasks remains challenging due to limited task-specific data and distribution shifts. While fine-tuning has proven transformative in natural language processing, best practices for adapting PDE foundation models remain underexplored. Although physics-informed training has successfully trained accurate solvers across a wide range of PDE problems, its potential for fine-tuning data-based foundation models has not been systematically studied. In this work, we introduce a physics-informed fine-tuning framework that adapts pre-trained PDE foundation models by incorporating physical constraints (PDE residuals and boundary conditions) directly into the fine-tuning objective. This enables effective adaptation in data-scarce regimes while promoting physical consistency. We evaluate our method on a downstream task composed of an unseen PDE class and compare it with data-driven finetuning counterparts. Our results demonstrate that physics-informed fine-tuning achieves competitive accuracy without requiring PDE solutions for training. Furthermore, a hybrid fine-tuning strategy yields superior generalization to out-of-distribution scenarios when only minimal training data is available. These findings establish physics-informed fine-tuning as a scalable and data-efficient paradigm, providing a physically interpretable pathway for adapting foundation models in scientific machine learning.

</details>


### [277] [TabKD: Tabular Knowledge Distillation through Interaction Diversity of Learned Feature Bins](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.15481)
*Shovon Niverd Pereira,Krishna Khadka,Yu Lei*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: TabKD：一种针对表格数据的无数据知识蒸馏方法，通过最大化特征交互覆盖来提升蒸馏效果


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有无数据知识蒸馏方法在表格数据上表现不佳，因为它们没有显式处理特征交互——这是表格模型编码预测知识的基本方式。需要解决特征交互多样性问题以实现有效的表格蒸馏。

Method: 提出TabKD方法：1）学习与教师决策边界对齐的自适应特征分箱；2）生成最大化成对交互覆盖的合成查询

Result: 在4个基准数据集和4种教师架构上，TabKD在16个配置中的14个取得了最高的师生一致性，优于5个最先进的基线方法。交互覆盖与蒸馏质量强相关，验证了核心假设。

Conclusion: 该工作确立了以交互为中心的探索作为表格模型提取的原则性框架，为隐私敏感的表格领域提供了有效的无数据模型压缩方法。

Abstract: Data-free knowledge distillation enables model compression without original training data, critical for privacy-sensitive tabular domains. However, existing methods does not perform well on tabular data because they do not explicitly address feature interactions, the fundamental way tabular models encode predictive knowledge. We identify interaction diversity, systematic coverage of feature combinations, as an essential requirement for effective tabular distillation. To operationalize this insight, we propose TabKD, which learns adaptive feature bins aligned with teacher decision boundaries, then generates synthetic queries that maximize pairwise interaction coverage. Across 4 benchmark datasets and 4 teacher architectures, TabKD achieves highest student-teacher agreement in 14 out of 16 configurations, outperforming 5 state-of-the-art baselines. We further show that interaction coverage strongly correlates with distillation quality, validating our core hypothesis. Our work establishes interaction-focused exploration as a principled framework for tabular model extraction.

</details>


### [278] [Grokking as a Variance-Limited Phase Transition: Spectral Gating and the Epsilon-Stability Threshold](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.15492)
*Pratyush Acharya,Habish Dhakal*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文揭示了AdamW优化器在模块化算术任务中通过"谱门控"机制实现grokking现象：泛化需要梯度方差积累以突破稳定性限制，进入尖锐的泛化解流形。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准优化理论难以解释grokking现象（训练收敛后很久才出现泛化）。现有几何研究关注缓慢漂移，但忽略了优化器噪声结构与损失曲面曲率的相互作用。

Method: 分析AdamW在模块化算术任务上的动力学，提出"谱门控"机制。通过消融研究识别三个复杂度区域：容量崩溃、方差限制和稳定性覆盖。

Result: 发现AdamW作为方差门控随机系统运作，grokking受稳定性条件约束。泛化解位于尖锐盆地，初始在低方差状态下不可达。各向同性噪声注入无法诱导grokking，需要自适应优化器的各向异性整流。

Conclusion: grokking需要自适应优化器的各向异性噪声整流，将噪声引导到解流形的切空间。挑战了算法任务中的"平坦最小值"假设，表明泛化需要特定的噪声结构。

Abstract: Standard optimization theories struggle to explain grokking, where generalization occurs long after training convergence. While geometric studies attribute this to slow drift, they often overlook the interaction between the optimizer's noise structure and landscape curvature. This work analyzes AdamW dynamics on modular arithmetic tasks, revealing a ``Spectral Gating'' mechanism that regulates the transition from memorization to generalization.
  We find that AdamW operates as a variance-gated stochastic system. Grokking is constrained by a stability condition: the generalizing solution resides in a sharp basin ($λ_{max}^H$) initially inaccessible under low-variance regimes. The ``delayed'' phase represents the accumulation of gradient variance required to lift the effective stability ceiling, permitting entry into this sharp manifold.
  Our ablation studies identify three complexity regimes: (1) \textbf{Capacity Collapse} ($P < 23$), where rank-deficiency prevents structural learning; (2) \textbf{The Variance-Limited Regime} ($P \approx 41$), where generalization waits for the spectral gate to open; and (3) \textbf{Stability Override} ($P > 67$), where memorization becomes dimensionally unstable. Furthermore, we challenge the "Flat Minima" hypothesis for algorithmic tasks, showing that isotropic noise injection fails to induce grokking. Generalization requires the \textit{anisotropic rectification} unique to adaptive optimizers, which directs noise into the tangent space of the solution manifold.

</details>


### [279] [Seeking SOTA: Time-Series Forecasting Must Adopt Taxonomy-Specific Evaluation to Dispel Illusory Gains](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.15506)
*Raeid Saqur,Christoph Bergmeir,Blanka Horvath,Daniel Schmidt,Frank Rudzicz,Terry Lyons*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 当前时间序列预测评估过度依赖具有强周期性的基准数据集，导致深度学习模型与经典方法相比优势不明显，建议采用更复杂的非平稳数据集并强制包含经典基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前AI/ML时间序列预测模型的评估实践存在问题，主要使用具有强周期性和季节性的基准数据集，这掩盖了真实进展，因为简单的经典方法在这些数据集上表现也很好。需要更全面的评估框架来反映模型在真实复杂场景下的能力。

Method: 通过分析现有基准数据集的特征，指出它们主要包含主导的自相关模式和季节周期，这些特征可以被简单的线性或统计模型有效捕捉。提出两个具体建议：(1) 用更广泛的非平稳性数据集替换或增强现有基准；(2) 要求所有深度学习提交必须包含适当的经典基线模型。

Result: 研究表明，在现有"标准"数据集上，复杂的深度学习架构通常不比经典方法表现更好，其边际改进可能无法证明计算开销和模型复杂度的增加是合理的。

Conclusion: 需要改革时间序列预测的评估实践，采用更具挑战性的数据集并强制包含经典基线，以确保报告的改进反映真正的科学方法进步，而不是基准选择偏向于擅长学习重复模式的模型。

Abstract: We argue that the current practice of evaluating AI/ML time-series forecasting models, predominantly on benchmarks characterized by strong, persistent periodicities and seasonalities, obscures real progress by overlooking the performance of efficient classical methods. We demonstrate that these "standard" datasets often exhibit dominant autocorrelation patterns and seasonal cycles that can be effectively captured by simpler linear or statistical models, rendering complex deep learning architectures frequently no more performant than their classical counterparts for these specific data characteristics, and raising questions as to whether any marginal improvements justify the significant increase in computational overhead and model complexity. We call on the community to (I) retire or substantially augment current benchmarks with datasets exhibiting a wider spectrum of non-stationarities, such as structural breaks, time-varying volatility, and concept drift, and less predictable dynamics drawn from diverse real-world domains, and (II) require every deep learning submission to include robust classical and simple baselines, appropriately chosen for the specific characteristics of the downstream tasks' time series. By doing so, we will help ensure that reported gains reflect genuine scientific methodological advances rather than artifacts of benchmark selection favoring models adept at learning repetitive patterns.

</details>


### [280] [Federated Learning of Binary Neural Networks: Enabling Low-Cost Inference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.15507)
*Nitin Priyadarshini Shankar,Soham Lahiri,Sheetal Kalyani,Saurav Prakash*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: FedBNN提出了一种面向联邦学习的旋转感知二值神经网络框架，通过将权重编码为单比特{+1,-1}，显著减少模型大小和计算开销，同时保持与实值模型相当的精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 联邦学习在边缘设备上部署DNN面临计算资源限制的挑战。传统后训练二值化虽然减小模型尺寸但导致严重精度损失，需要在保持隐私保护的同时优化内存占用和计算效率。

Method: 提出FedBNN框架，在本地训练期间直接学习二值表示，将每个权重编码为单比特{+1,-1}而非32位浮点数，采用旋转感知的二值神经网络设计。

Result: 在多个基准数据集上的评估表明，FedBNN显著减少了资源消耗（内存需求和推理时的FLOPs），同时性能与使用实值模型的现有联邦学习方法相当。

Conclusion: FedBNN通过直接在联邦学习中学习二值表示，有效解决了边缘设备资源限制问题，在保持隐私保护的同时实现了模型大小和计算效率的优化。

Abstract: Federated Learning (FL) preserves privacy by distributing training across devices. However, using DNNs is computationally intensive at the low-powered edge during inference. Edge deployment demands models that simultaneously optimize memory footprint and computational efficiency, a dilemma where conventional DNNs fail by exceeding resource limits. Traditional post-training binarization reduces model size but suffers from severe accuracy loss due to quantization errors. To address these challenges, we propose FedBNN, a rotation-aware binary neural network framework that learns binary representations directly during local training. By encoding each weight as a single bit $\{+1, -1\}$ instead of a $32$-bit float, FedBNN shrinks the model footprint, significantly reducing runtime (during inference) FLOPs and memory requirements in comparison to federated methods using real models. Evaluations across multiple benchmark datasets demonstrate that FedBNN significantly reduces resource consumption while performing similarly to existing federated methods using real-valued models.

</details>


### [281] [Not All Invariants Are Equal: Curating Training Data to Accelerate Program Verification with SLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.15510)
*Ido Pinto,Yizhak Yisrael Elboher,Haoze Wu,Nina Narodytska,Guy Katz*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出Wonda数据清洗流程，从验证器生成的原始不变量中提取高质量训练数据，用于微调小型语言模型，使其在循环不变量生成任务上达到接近GPT-5.2的性能水平。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 循环不变量合成是程序自动验证的关键瓶颈。虽然大语言模型有潜力解决此问题，但在困难实例上经常生成无效或计算效率低的不变量。微调是自然的解决方案，但获取高质量训练数据仍然是一个开放挑战。

Method: 提出Wonda数据清洗流程：1）形式化高质量训练不变量的要求；2）通过AST规范化处理噪声数据；3）使用LLM驱动的语义重写和增强，并提供可证明的质量保证。使用该清洗后的数据微调小型语言模型。

Result: 微调后的40亿参数模型匹配GPT-OSS-120B基线的效用，接近最先进的GPT-5.2性能，且没有推理时间开销。在InvBench评估套件的挑战性实例上，正确性和加速率提高一倍，验证任务的虚拟最佳性能提升达14.2%。

Conclusion: 通过精心设计的数据清洗流程可以从验证器生成的原始不变量中提取高质量训练信号，使小型语言模型在循环不变量生成任务上达到接近最先进大模型的性能，为程序验证提供了高效实用的解决方案。

Abstract: The synthesis of inductive loop invariants is a critical bottleneck in automated program verification. While Large Language Models (LLMs) show promise in mitigating this issue, they often fail on hard instances, generating invariants that are invalid or computationally ineffective. While fine-tuning is a natural route to mitigate this limitation, obtaining high-quality training data for invariant generation remains an open challenge. We present a rigorous data curation pipeline designed to extract high-quality training signals from raw verifier-generated invariants. First, we formalize the properties required for a high-quality training invariant. Second, we propose Wonda, a pipeline that refines noisy data via AST-based normalization, followed by LLM-driven semantic rewriting and augmentation with provable quality guarantees. We demonstrate that fine-tuning Small Language Models (SLMs) on this curated dataset result in consistent and significant performance gain. In particular, a fine-tuned 4B parameter model matches the utility of a GPT-OSS-120B baseline and approaches the state-of-the-art GPT-5.2, without incurring reasoning-time overhead. On challenging instances from the recent InvBench evaluation suite, our approach doubles the invariant correctness and speedup rates of base models; and improves their Virtual Best Performance (VBP) rates on the verification task by up to 14.2%.

</details>


### [282] [Building Trust in PINNs: Error Estimation through Finite Difference Methods](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.15526)
*Aleksander Krasowski,René P. Klausen,Aycan Celik,Sebastian Lapuschkin,Wojciech Samek,Jonas Naujoks*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出一种轻量级后处理方法，为PINN预测生成逐点误差估计，通过求解误差方程来量化PINN预测与真实解的偏差。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: PINN虽然灵活，但缺乏对其预测质量的可信度评估，无法提供预测偏离真实解的具体信息，这限制了用户对其预测结果的信任。

Method: 利用线性PDE中PINN近似与真实解之间的误差满足与原问题相同微分算子的特性，将PINN的PDE残差作为源项，使用有限差分法数值求解误差方程，无需真实解的先验知识。

Result: 在多个基准PDE上测试，该方法能以低计算成本生成准确的误差分布图，实现了对PINN预测的针对性、可解释性验证。

Conclusion: 该方法为PINN提供了实用的误差估计工具，增强了PINN预测的可信度和可解释性，有助于识别预测错误的位置和程度。

Abstract: Physics-informed neural networks (PINNs) constitute a flexible deep learning approach for solving partial differential equations (PDEs), which model phenomena ranging from heat conduction to quantum mechanical systems. Despite their flexibility, PINNs offer limited insight into how their predictions deviate from the true solution, hindering trust in their prediction quality. We propose a lightweight post-hoc method that addresses this gap by producing pointwise error estimates for PINN predictions, which offer a natural form of explanation for such models, identifying not just whether a prediction is wrong, but where and by how much. For linear partial differential equations, the error between a PINN approximation and the true solution satisfies the same differential operator as the original problem, but driven by the PINN's PDE residual as its source term. We solve this error equation numerically using finite difference methods requiring no knowledge of the true solution. Evaluated on several benchmark PDEs, our method yields accurate error maps at low computational cost, enabling targeted and interpretable validation of PINNs.

</details>


### [283] [Vib2ECG: A Paired Chest-Lead SCG-ECG Dataset and Benchmark for ECG Reconstruction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.15539)
*Guorui Lu,Xiaohui Cai,Todor Stefanov,Qinyu Chen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: Vib2ECG：首个配对多通道心电-振动信号数据集，用于从低成本振动信号重建12导联心电图，并提出基准模型


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统12导联心电图设备复杂昂贵，难以长期日常监测。现有方法只能重建肢体导联，而临床诊断需要包含胸导联的多导联心电图

Method: 创建Vib2ECG数据集（17名受试者，包含完整12导联心电图和6个胸导联位置的IMU振动信号），使用轻量级364K参数U-Net进行基准测试

Result: 实验证明可以从振动信号重建不同位置的心电信号，但发现模型存在幻觉现象（在没有电活动的区域生成心电图波形）

Conclusion: 通过低成本IMU传感器获取振动信号来预测胸导联心电图是可行的，为移动设备友好的心电监测提供了新途径，并揭示了心脏电-机械活动的空间关系

Abstract: Twelve-lead electrocardiography (ECG) is essential for cardiovascular diagnosis, but its long-term acquisition in daily life is constrained by complex and costly hardware. Recent efforts have explored reconstructing ECG from low-cost cardiac vibrational signals such as seismocardiography (SCG), however, due to the lack of a dataset, current methods are limited to limb leads, while clinical diagnosis requires multi-lead ECG, including chest leads. In this work, we propose Vib2ECG, the first paired, multi-channel electro-mechanical cardiac signal dataset, which includes complete twelve-lead ECGs and vibrational signals acquired by inertial measurement units (IMUs) at six chest-lead positions from 17 subjects. Based on this dataset, we also provide a benchmark. Experimental results demonstrate the feasibility of reconstructing electrical cardiac signals at variable locations from vibrational signals using a lightweight 364 K-parameter U-Net. Furthermore, we observe a hallucination phenomenon in the model, where ECG waveforms are generated in regions where no corresponding electrical activity is present. We analyze the causes of this phenomenon and propose potential directions for mitigation. This study demonstrates the feasibility of mobile-device-friendly ECG monitoring through chest-lead ECG prediction from low-cost vibrational signals acquired using IMU sensors. It expands the application of cardiac vibrational signals and provides new insights into the spatial relationship between cardiac electrical and mechanical activities with spatial location variation.

</details>


### [284] [Bridging Local and Global Knowledge: Cascaded Mixture-of-Experts Learning for Near-Shortest Path Routing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.15541)
*Yung-Fu Chen,Anish Arora*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出Ca-MoE（级联专家混合）架构解决稀疏网络中的路由问题，通过两级专家系统实现自适应推理，在保持计算效率的同时提升稀疏网络中的路由准确性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于局部特征的深度学习模型在密集欧几里得图中表现良好，但在稀疏网络中泛化能力差，因为稀疏网络的拓扑不规则性需要更广泛的结构感知。

Method: 采用模块化的两级Ca-MoE架构：下层专家依赖局部特征，上层专家依赖全局特征；实现自适应推理，仅当下层专家决策质量不足时才触发上层专家；结合在线元学习策略，支持独立专家微调，并使用稳定性优先的更新机制防止灾难性遗忘。

Result: 在稀疏网络中，Ca-MoE路由比单专家基线准确率提升高达29.1%；在不同图密度下，性能保持在理论上限的1%-6%范围内。

Conclusion: Ca-MoE通过自适应触发全局特征专家，有效解决了稀疏网络中的路由泛化问题，在保持计算效率的同时显著提升了路由准确性。

Abstract: While deep learning models that leverage local features have demonstrated significant potential for near-optimal routing in dense Euclidean graphs, they struggle to generalize well in sparse networks where topological irregularities require broader structural awareness. To address this limitation, we train a Cascaded Mixture of Experts (Ca-MoE) to solve the all-pairs near-shortest path (APNSP) routing problem. Our Ca-MoE is a modular two-tier architecture that supports the decision-making for forwarder selection with lower-tier experts relying on local features and upper-tier experts relying on global features. It performs adaptive inference wherein the upper-tier experts are triggered only when the lower-tier ones do not suffice to achieve adequate decision quality. Computational efficiency is thus achieved by escalating model capacity only when necessitated by topological complexity, and parameter redundancy is avoided. Furthermore, we incorporate an online meta-learning strategy that facilitates independent expert fine-tuning and utilizes a stability-focused update mechanism to prevent catastrophic forgetting as new graph environments are encountered. Experimental evaluations demonstrate that Ca-MoE routing improves accuracy by up to 29.1% in sparse networks compared to single-expert baselines and maintains performance within 1%-6% of the theoretical upper bound across diverse graph densities.

</details>


### [285] [The PokeAgent Challenge: Competitive and Long-Context Learning at Scale](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.15563)
*Seth Karten,Jake Grigsby,Tersoo Upaa,Junik Bae,Seonghun Hong,Hyunyoung Jeong,Jaeyoon Jung,Kun Kerdthaisong,Gyungbo Kim,Hyeokgi Kim,Yujin Kim,Eunju Kwon,Dongyu Liu,Patrick Mariglia,Sangyeon Park,Benedikt Schink,Xianwei Shi,Anthony Sistilli,Joseph Twin,Arian Urdu,Matin Urdu,Qiao Wang,Ling Wu,Wenli Zhang,Kunsheng Zhou,Stephanie Milani,Kiran Vodrahalli,Amy Zhang,Fei Fang,Yuke Zhu,Chi Jin*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: PokeAgent Challenge是一个基于宝可梦游戏的大规模决策研究基准，包含对战和速通两个赛道，用于评估AI在部分可观测、博弈论推理和长时程规划方面的能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 目前缺乏能够同时评估部分可观测性、博弈论推理和长时程规划的现实基准。宝可梦游戏环境提供了这些挑战的天然测试平台，可以推动强化学习和大语言模型研究的发展。

Method: 构建了两个互补赛道：对战赛道（提供2000万+对战轨迹数据集和启发式、强化学习、大语言模型基线）和速通赛道（首个RPG速通标准化评估框架，包含开源多智能体编排系统）。

Result: NeurIPS 2025竞赛吸引了100多支团队参与，结果显示通用模型（大语言模型）、专用模型（强化学习）和精英人类玩家之间存在显著差距。宝可梦对战能力与标准大语言模型基准几乎正交。

Conclusion: PokeAgent Challenge是一个未解决的基准，能够测量现有基准无法捕捉的能力，推动强化学习和大语言模型研究发展，现已转变为持续更新的基准平台。

Abstract: We present the PokeAgent Challenge, a large-scale benchmark for decision-making research built on Pokemon's multi-agent battle system and expansive role-playing game (RPG) environment. Partial observability, game-theoretic reasoning, and long-horizon planning remain open problems for frontier AI, yet few benchmarks stress all three simultaneously under realistic conditions. PokeAgent targets these limitations at scale through two complementary tracks: our Battling Track, which calls for strategic reasoning and generalization under partial observability in competitive Pokemon battles, and our Speedrunning Track, which requires long-horizon planning and sequential decision-making in the Pokemon RPG. Our Battling Track supplies a dataset of 20M+ battle trajectories alongside a suite of heuristic, RL, and LLM-based baselines capable of high-level competitive play. Our Speedrunning Track provides the first standardized evaluation framework for RPG speedrunning, including an open-source multi-agent orchestration system for modular, reproducible comparisons of harness-based LLM approaches. Our NeurIPS 2025 competition validates both the quality of our resources and the research community's interest in Pokemon, with over 100 teams competing across both tracks and winning solutions detailed in our paper. Participant submissions and our baselines reveal considerable gaps between generalist (LLM), specialist (RL), and elite human performance. Analysis against the BenchPress evaluation matrix shows that Pokemon battling is nearly orthogonal to standard LLM benchmarks, measuring capabilities not captured by existing suites and positioning Pokemon as an unsolved benchmark that can drive RL and LLM research forward. We transition to a living benchmark with a live leaderboard for Battling and self-contained evaluation for Speedrunning at https://pokeagentchallenge.com.

</details>


### [286] [Mamba-3: Improved Sequence Modeling using State Space Principles](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.15569)
*Aakash Lahoti,Kevin Y. Li,Berlin Chen,Caitlin Wang,Aviv Bick,J. Zico Kolter,Tri Dao,Albert Gu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: Mamba-3通过状态空间模型视角改进线性模型，结合更复杂的递归、复数状态更新和MIMO架构，在保持高效推理的同时显著提升模型性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前Transformer模型推理成本高（二次计算和线性内存），而现有线性模型在追求算法效率时牺牲了模型质量和能力，特别是在状态跟踪等任务上表现不佳，且理论上的线性推理在实践中硬件效率不高。

Method: 基于状态空间模型视角引入三个核心改进：1) 从SSM离散化推导的更复杂递归；2) 支持更丰富状态跟踪的复数状态更新规则；3) 多输入多输出(MIMO)架构，在不增加解码延迟的情况下提升性能。结合架构优化，形成Mamba-3模型。

Result: 在1.5B规模上，Mamba-3相比次优模型(Gated DeltaNet)平均下游准确率提升0.6个百分点，MIMO变体进一步提升1.2个百分点，总计1.8点提升。在状态大小实验中，Mamba-3使用一半状态大小即可达到与Mamba-2相当的困惑度，推进了性能-效率的帕累托前沿。

Conclusion: Mamba-3通过推理优先的设计视角，成功解决了线性模型在质量与效率之间的权衡问题，在检索、状态跟踪和语言建模任务上取得显著进步，为高效推理模型提供了新的发展方向。

Abstract: Scaling inference-time compute has emerged as an important driver of LLM performance, making inference efficiency a central focus of model design alongside model quality. While the current Transformer-based models deliver strong model quality, their quadratic compute and linear memory make inference expensive. This has spurred the development of sub-quadratic models with reduced linear compute and constant memory requirements. However, many recent linear models trade off model quality and capability for algorithmic efficiency, failing on tasks such as state tracking. Moreover, their theoretically linear inference remains hardware-inefficient in practice. Guided by an inference-first perspective, we introduce three core methodological improvements inspired by the state space model (SSM) viewpoint of linear models. We combine: (1) a more expressive recurrence derived from SSM discretization, (2) a complex-valued state update rule that enables richer state tracking, and (3) a multi-input, multi-output (MIMO) formulation for better model performance without increasing decode latency. Together with architectural refinements, our Mamba-3 model achieves significant gains across retrieval, state-tracking, and downstream language modeling tasks. At the 1.5B scale, Mamba-3 improves average downstream accuracy by 0.6 percentage points compared to the next best model (Gated DeltaNet), with Mamba-3's MIMO variant further improving accuracy by another 1.2 points for a total 1.8 point gain. Across state-size experiments, Mamba-3 achieves comparable perplexity to Mamba-2 despite using half of its predecessor's state size. Our evaluations demonstrate Mamba-3's ability to advance the performance-efficiency Pareto frontier.

</details>


### [287] [Physics-Informed Neural Systems for the Simulation of EUV Electromagnetic Wave Diffraction from a Lithography Mask](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.15584)
*Vasiliy A. Es'kin,Egor V. Ivanov*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出基于物理信息神经网络(PINNs)和神经算子(NOs)的混合波导神经算子(WGNO)，用于加速极紫外光刻掩模衍射问题的求解，在保持精度的同时显著减少计算时间。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统数值求解器在计算极紫外光刻掩模衍射问题时计算成本高，影响掩模设计和优化流程的效率。需要开发更高效的求解方法以加速下一代光刻掩模的设计过程。

Method: 提出混合波导神经算子(WGNO)，结合波导方法和神经网络，用神经网络替代波导方法中最耗计算资源的组件。同时比较PINNs和神经算子的性能，在13.5nm和11.2nm波长下评估精度。

Result: 在2D和3D实际掩模上的数值实验表明，PINNs和神经算子达到竞争性精度，同时预测时间显著减少。WGNO架构达到最先进性能，对未见问题参数具有良好的泛化能力。

Conclusion: 提出的神经算子方法为加速下一代光刻掩模设计和优化流程提供了高效解决方案，在保持精度的同时大幅提升计算效率，具有实际应用价值。

Abstract: Physics-informed neural networks (PINNs) and neural operators (NOs) for solving the problem of diffraction of Extreme Ultraviolet (EUV) electromagnetic waves from contemporary lithography masks are presented. A novel hybrid Waveguide Neural Operator (WGNO) is introduced, based on a waveguide method with its most computationally expensive components replaced by a neural network. To evaluate performance, the accuracy and inference time of PINNs and NOs are compared against modern numerical solvers for a series of problems with known exact solutions. The emphasis is placed on investigation of solution accuracy by considered artificial neural systems for 13.5 nm and 11.2 nm wavelengths. Numerical experiments on realistic 2D and 3D masks demonstrate that PINNs and neural operators achieve competitive accuracy and significantly reduced prediction times, with the proposed WGNO architecture reaching state-of-the-art performance. The presented neural operator has pronounced generalizing properties, meaning that for unseen problem parameters it delivers a solution accuracy close to that for parameters seen in the training dataset. These results provide a highly efficient solution for accelerating the design and optimization workflows of next-generation lithography masks.

</details>


### [288] [Effective Distillation to Hybrid xLSTM Architectures](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.15590)
*Lukas Hauzenberger,Niklas Schmidinger,Thomas Schmied,Anamaria-Roberta Hartl,David Stap,Pieter-Jan Hoedt,Maximilian Beck,Sebastian Böck,Günter Klambauer,Sepp Hochreiter*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文提出了一种无损蒸馏方法，将二次注意力机制的LLM蒸馏到亚二次线性化架构（xLSTM）中，通过合并线性化专家实现性能恢复甚至超越教师模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的将二次注意力LLM蒸馏到亚二次线性化架构的方法往往无法在多种下游任务上匹配教师模型的性能，需要开发更有效的蒸馏方法来创建更节能、成本效益更高的Transformer替代方案。

Method: 提出无损蒸馏管道，定义基于容忍度校正的Win-and-Tie率评估标准；引入额外的合并阶段，将单独线性化的专家组合成单一模型；在Llama、Qwen和Olmo系列模型上进行基础模型和指令调优模型的蒸馏。

Result: xLSTM学生模型在多数设置下恢复了教师模型的大部分性能，甚至在某些下游任务上超越了教师模型，为实现更节能、成本效益更高的LLM替代方案迈出重要一步。

Conclusion: 提出的无损蒸馏管道有效实现了从二次注意力LLM到亚二次线性化架构的性能转移，xLSTM学生模型能够匹配甚至超越教师模型性能，为开发更高效的LLM替代方案提供了重要进展。

Abstract: There have been numerous attempts to distill quadratic attention-based large language models (LLMs) into sub-quadratic linearized architectures. However, despite extensive research, such distilled models often fail to match the performance of their teacher LLMs on various downstream tasks. We set out the goal of lossless distillation, which we define in terms of tolerance-corrected Win-and-Tie rates between student and teacher on sets of tasks. To this end, we introduce an effective distillation pipeline for xLSTM-based students. We propose an additional merging stage, where individually linearized experts are combined into a single model. We show the effectiveness of this pipeline by distilling base and instruction-tuned models from the Llama, Qwen, and Olmo families. In many settings, our xLSTM-based students recover most of the teacher's performance, and even exceed it on some downstream tasks. Our contributions are an important step towards more energy-efficient and cost-effective replacements for transformer-based LLMs.

</details>


### [289] [Robust and Computationally Efficient Linear Contextual Bandits under Adversarial Corruption and Heavy-Tailed Noise](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.15596)
*Naoto Tani,Futoshi Futami*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了一种基于在线镜像下降的计算高效算法，用于处理对抗性污染和重尾噪声的线性上下文赌博机问题，将计算成本从O(t log T)降低到O(1)每轮。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法同时处理对抗性污染和重尾噪声时，依赖于有限方差假设且计算效率低下。需要一种计算高效的算法，能够在重尾噪声（仅具有有限(1+ε)阶矩）和对抗性污染下保持鲁棒性。

Method: 基于在线镜像下降（OMD）的计算高效算法。算法不需要事先知道噪声矩界或污染总量，通过自适应机制处理重尾噪声和对抗性污染。

Result: 算法实现了O(1)每轮的计算复杂度（相比现有方法的O(t log T)）。建立了包含噪声(1+ε)阶矩界和污染总量的加性遗憾界。当ε=1时恢复有限方差假设下的现有保证；无污染时匹配重尾噪声线性上下文赌博机的最佳已知速率。

Conclusion: 提出了一种计算高效的鲁棒算法，能够同时处理对抗性污染和重尾噪声，无需先验知识，保证亚线性遗憾，显著改进了现有方法的计算效率。

Abstract: We study linear contextual bandits under adversarial corruption and heavy-tailed noise with finite $(1+ε)$-th moments for some $ε\in (0,1]$. Existing work that addresses both adversarial corruption and heavy-tailed noise relies on a finite variance (i.e., finite second-moment) assumption and suffers from computational inefficiency. We propose a computationally efficient algorithm based on online mirror descent that achieves robustness to both adversarial corruption and heavy-tailed noise. While the existing algorithm incurs $\mathcal{O}(t\log T)$ computational cost, our algorithm reduces this to $\mathcal{O}(1)$ per round. We establish an additive regret bound consisting of a term depending on the $(1+ε)$-moment bound of the noise and a term depending on the total amount of corruption. In particular, when $ε= 1$, our result recovers existing guarantees under finite-variance assumptions. When no corruption is present, it matches the best-known rates for linear contextual bandits with heavy-tailed noise. Moreover, the algorithm requires no prior knowledge of the noise moment bound or the total amount of corruption and still guarantees sublinear regret.

</details>


### [290] [SmartSearch: How Ranking Beats Structure for Conversational Memory Retrieval](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.15599)
*Jesper Derehag,Carlos Calva,Timmy Ghiurau*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: SmartSearch是一个完全确定性的对话记忆检索系统，无需LLM结构化或学习检索策略，通过NER加权子串匹配、规则实体发现和混合排序实现高效检索。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有对话记忆系统过度依赖LLM结构化处理和学习检索策略，作者认为这些都不是必要的，希望开发更简单高效的检索方法。

Method: 采用完全确定性流水线：1) NER加权子串匹配用于召回；2) 规则实体发现用于多跳扩展；3) CrossEncoder+ColBERT排序融合阶段（唯一学习组件），在CPU上运行约650ms。

Result: 在LoCoMo上达到93.5%，在LongMemEval-S上达到88.4%，超过所有已知记忆系统，同时比全上下文基线少用8.5倍token。

Conclusion: SmartSearch证明无需复杂的LLM结构化或学习检索策略即可实现高效对话记忆检索，通过简单确定性方法获得优异性能。

Abstract: Recent conversational memory systems invest heavily in LLM-based structuring at ingestion time and learned retrieval policies at query time. We show that neither is necessary. SmartSearch retrieves from raw, unstructured conversation history using a fully deterministic pipeline: NER-weighted substring matching for recall, rule-based entity discovery for multi-hop expansion, and a CrossEncoder+ColBERT rank fusion stage -- the only learned component -- running on CPU in ~650ms. Oracle analysis on two benchmarks identifies a compilation bottleneck: retrieval recall reaches 98.6%, but without intelligent ranking only 22.5% of gold evidence survives truncation to the token budget. With score-adaptive truncation and no per-dataset tuning, SmartSearch achieves 93.5% on LoCoMo and 88.4% on LongMemEval-S, exceeding all known memory systems under the same evaluation protocol on both benchmarks while using 8.5x fewer tokens than full-context baselines.

</details>


### [291] [HorizonMath: Measuring AI Progress Toward Mathematical Discovery with Automatic Verification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.15617)
*Erik Y. Wang,Sumeet Motwani,James V. Roggeveen,Eliot Hodges,Dulhan Jayalath,Charles London,Kalyan Ramakrishnan,Flaviu Cipcigan,Philip Torr,Alessandro Abate*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: HorizonMath是一个包含100多个未解决数学问题的基准测试，专注于验证简单但需要深刻数学洞察的问题，用于评估AI是否能在数学研究中做出新颖贡献。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前大语言模型在数学推理方面已有显著进展，但能否进行真正新颖的数学研究仍存在争议且研究不足。现有研究级基准测试依赖形式化证明验证或人工评审，难以规模化。

Method: 创建HorizonMath基准测试，包含8个计算和应用数学领域的100多个未解决问题，配备开源评估框架进行自动化验证。这些问题的特点是发现困难但验证简单高效。

Result: 大多数最先进模型在HorizonMath上得分接近0%。GPT 5.4 Pro为两个问题提出了改进现有最佳结果的解决方案，可能代表了新颖贡献（待专家评审）。

Conclusion: HorizonMath作为开放挑战和社区资源发布，其中未解决问题的正确解决方案可能构成数学文献中的新颖结果，为评估AI数学研究能力提供了新平台。

Abstract: Can AI make progress on important, unsolved mathematical problems? Large language models are now capable of sophisticated mathematical and scientific reasoning, but whether they can perform novel research is still widely debated and underexplored. We introduce HorizonMath, a benchmark of over 100 predominantly unsolved problems spanning 8 domains in computational and applied mathematics, paired with an open-source evaluation framework for automated verification. Our benchmark targets a class of problems where discovery is hard, requiring meaningful mathematical insight, but verification is computationally efficient and simple. Because these solutions are unknown, HorizonMath is immune to data contamination, and most state-of-the-art models score near 0%. Existing research-level benchmarks instead rely on formal proof verification or manual review, both of which are expensive to scale. Using this platform, we find two problems for which GPT 5.4 Pro proposes solutions that improve on the best-known published results, representing potential novel contributions (pending expert review). We release HorizonMath as an open challenge and a growing community resource, where correct solutions to problems in the unsolved problem classes could constitute novel results in the mathematical literature.

</details>


<div id='stat.ML'></div>

# stat.ML [[Back]](#toc)

### [292] [A Hybrid Tsallis-Polarization Impurity Measure for Decision Trees: Theoretical Foundations and Empirical Evaluation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.13241)
*Edouard Lansiaux,Idriss Jairi,Hayfa Zgaya-Biau*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 提出ITC混合不纯度度量，结合Tsallis熵与指数极化分量，在理论严谨性与计算效率间取得平衡，实验显示简单参数化度量表现最佳，ITC提供理论保证的竞争性替代方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有不纯度度量往往在理论严谨性和计算效率之间难以兼顾，要么牺牲理论严谨性追求计算效率，要么反之。需要一种既能保持数学原理性又能平衡实际计算需求的混合度量方法。

Method: 提出集成Tsallis组合(ITC)，将归一化Tsallis熵与指数极化分量相结合。Tsallis熵提供信息论基础，极化分量增强对分布不对称性的敏感性。建立关键理论性质：在明确参数条件下的凹性、适当边界条件、与经典度量的联系，并为混合策略提供严格论证。

Result: 在7个基准数据集上对23种不纯度度量进行五折重复比较评估，简单参数化度量(Tsallis α=0.5)获得最高平均准确率(91.17%)，ITC变体获得竞争性结果(88.38-89.16%)且有更强理论保证。统计分析(Friedman检验：χ²=3.89，p=0.692)显示顶级性能者间无显著全局差异。

Conclusion: ITC的价值在于其坚实的理论基础：在适当条件下证明的凹性、灵活的参数化(α, β, γ)和O(K)计算效率，使其成为理论保证至关重要时的严谨、可泛化替代方案。提供了基于应用优先级的度量选择指南，并开源实现以促进可复现性和进一步研究。

Abstract: We introduce the Integrated Tsallis Combination (ITC), a hybrid impurity measure for decision tree learning that combines normalized Tsallis entropy with an exponential polarization component. While many existing measures sacrifice theoretical soundness for computational efficiency or vice versa, ITC provides a mathematically principled framework that balances both aspects. The core innovation lies in the complementarity between Tsallis entropy's information-theoretic foundations and the polarization component's sensitivity to distributional asymmetry. We establish key theoretical properties-concavity under explicit parameter conditions, proper boundary conditions, and connections to classical measures-and provide a rigorous justification for the hybridization strategy. Through an extensive comparative evaluation on seven benchmark datasets comparing 23 impurity measures with five-fold repetition, we show that simple parametric measures (Tsallis $α=0.5$) achieve the highest average accuracy ($91.17\%$), while ITC variants yield competitive results ($88.38-89.16\%$) with strong theoretical guarantees. Statistical analysis (Friedman test: $χ^2=3.89$, $p=0.692$) reveals no significant global differences among top performers, indicating practical equivalence for many applications. ITC's value resides in its solid theoretical grounding-proven concavity under suitable conditions, flexible parameterization ($α$, $β$, $γ$), and computational efficiency $O(K)$-making it a rigorous, generalizable alternative when theoretical guarantees are paramount. We provide guidelines for measure selection based on application priorities and release an open-source implementation to foster reproducibility and further research.

</details>


### [293] [Filtered Spectral Projection for Quantum Principal Component Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.13441)
*Sk Mujaffar Hossain,Satadeep Bhattacharjee*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 量子主成分分析(qPCA)通常关注特征值提取，但实际应用中更常需要将数据投影到主导谱子空间。本文提出FSPA算法，绕过显式特征值估计，直接进行谱投影，在保持谱结构的同时提高效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统qPCA方法专注于提取密度算子的特征值和特征向量，但在许多实际应用中，真正的目标是将数据投影到主导谱子空间。显式特征值估计可能是不必要的计算开销，特别是在小间隙和近简并情况下。

Method: 提出过滤谱投影算法(FSPA)，该算法绕过显式特征值估计，直接放大与主导主成分子空间的任何非零初始重叠。算法在振幅编码的中心数据中，将系综密度矩阵与协方差矩阵等同；对于非中心数据，推导特征值交错边界来量化与标准PCA的偏差。

Result: FSPA在保持谱结构的同时，在小间隙和近简并情况下保持鲁棒性，且不会在没有偏置的情况下引入人为对称破缺。在乳腺癌威斯康星和手写数字等基准数据集上的数值实验表明，只要投影质量得到保持，下游性能保持稳定。

Conclusion: 在广泛的qPCA设置中，谱投影是基本原语，显式特征值估计通常是不必要的。FSPA提供了一种更高效的投影优先框架，适用于实际量子机器学习应用。

Abstract: Quantum principal component analysis (qPCA) is commonly formulated as the extraction of eigenvalues and eigenvectors of a covariance-encoded density operator. Yet in many qPCA settings, the practical objective is simpler: projecting data onto the dominant spectral subspace. In this work, we introduce a projection-first framework, the Filtered Spectral Projection Algorithm (FSPA), which bypasses explicit eigenvalue estimation while preserving the essential spectral structure. FSPA amplifies any nonzero warm-start overlap with the leading principal subspace and remains robust in small-gap and near-degenerate regimes without inducing artificial symmetry breaking in the absence of bias. To connect this approach to classical datasets, we show that for amplitude-encoded centered data, the ensemble density matrix $ρ=\sum_i p_i|ψ_i\rangle\langleψ_i|$ coincides with the covariance matrix. For uncentered data, $ρ$ corresponds to PCA without centering, and we derive eigenvalue interlacing bounds quantifying the deviation from standard PCA. We further show that ensembles of quantum states admit an equivalent centered covariance interpretation. Numerical demonstrations on benchmark datasets, including Breast Cancer Wisconsin and handwritten Digits, show that downstream performance remains stable whenever projection quality is preserved. These results suggest that, in a broad class of qPCA settings, spectral projection is the essential primitive, and explicit eigenvalue estimation is often unnecessary.

</details>


### [294] [Standard Acquisition Is Sufficient for Asynchronous Bayesian Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.13501)
*Ben Riegler,James Odgers,Vincent Fortuin*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 异步贝叶斯优化中，标准采集函数（如上置信界）在理论上可以达到与顺序汤普森采样相当的保证，无需复杂的多样性强制方法，现有方法忽略了中间后验更新这一关键因素。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 挑战异步贝叶斯优化领域的一个基本前提：现有方法认为标准采集函数会导致冗余和重复查询，因此提出了复杂的解决方案来强制查询多样性。本文旨在验证这一前提是否正确。

Method: 对异步贝叶斯优化进行概念分析，揭示现有方法忽略了中间后验更新的关键作用。通过理论分析证明标准采集函数（如上置信界）可以达到与顺序汤普森采样相当的保证。通过广泛的实验验证简单标准采集函数与专门构建的异步方法的性能对比。

Result: 理论分析表明标准采集函数在异步设置下可以达到与顺序汤普森采样相当的保证。实验结果显示，简单的标准采集函数在合成和现实世界任务中匹配或优于专门构建的异步方法，而强制多样性的方法在异步设置中可能因过度探索而降低性能。

Conclusion: 异步贝叶斯优化中，标准采集函数本身已足够避免冗余查询，无需复杂的多样性强制机制。中间后验更新是关键因素，现有方法忽略了这一点。简单标准方法在实际应用中表现优异。

Abstract: Asynchronous Bayesian optimization is widely used for gradient-free optimization in domains with independent parallel experiments and varying evaluation times. Existing methods posit that standard acquisitions lead to redundant and repeated queries, proposing complex solutions to enforce diversity in queries. Challenging this fundamental premise, we show that methods, like the Upper Confidence Bound, can in fact achieve theoretical guarantees essentially equivalent to those of sequential Thompson sampling. A conceptual analysis of asynchronous Bayesian optimization reveals that existing works neglect intermediate posterior updates, which we find to be generally sufficient to avoid redundant queries. Further investigation shows that by penalizing busy locations, diversity-enforcing methods can over-explore in asynchronous settings, reducing their performance. Our extensive experiments demonstrate that simple standard acquisition functions match or outperform purpose-built asynchronous methods across synthetic and real-world tasks.

</details>


### [295] [Robust Automatic Differentiation of Square-Root Kalman Filters via Gramian Differentials](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.13559)
*Adrien Corenflos*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 论文提出了一种通过Gramian恒等式直接计算平方根卡尔曼滤波中QR分解梯度的新方法，解决了传统反向传播中的梯度未定义和发散问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 平方根卡尔曼滤波在状态空间模型参数学习中很实用，但对其核心QR分解操作进行反向传播时存在两个问题：1）当m>n时半正交因子不唯一导致梯度未定义；2）标准雅可比公式涉及逆矩阵，在输入矩阵秩亏时发散。

Method: 观察到所有与学习相关的滤波器输出仅依赖于输入矩阵的Gramian MM^⊤，因此即使三角化不光滑，复合损失在M中也是光滑的。基于Gramian恒等式的微分推导闭式链式法则，并通过列空间项（基于Moore-Penrose伪逆）和零空间校正的两分量分解扩展到秩亏输入。

Result: 推导出的闭式链式法则对卡尔曼对数边际似然和滤波矩是精确的，能够处理秩亏输入，避免了传统方法中的梯度未定义和发散问题。

Conclusion: 通过Gramian恒等式直接计算梯度的方法为平方根卡尔曼滤波的参数学习提供了稳定可靠的梯度计算方案，解决了传统反向传播中的数值稳定性问题。

Abstract: Square-root Kalman filters propagate state covariances in Cholesky-factor form for numerical stability, and are a natural target for gradient-based parameter learning in state-space models. Their core operation, triangularization of a matrix $M \in \mathbb{R}^{n \times m}$, is computed via a QR decomposition in practice, but naively differentiating through it causes two problems: the semi-orthogonal factor is non-unique when $m > n$, yielding undefined gradients; and the standard Jacobian formula involves inverses, which diverges when $M$ is rank-deficient. Both are resolved by the observation that all filter outputs relevant to learning depend on the input matrix only through the Gramian $MM^\top$, so the composite loss is smooth in $M$ even where the triangularization is not. We derive a closed-form chain-rule directly from the differential of this Gramian identity, prove it exact for the Kalman log-marginal likelihood and filtered moments, and extend it to rank-deficient inputs via a two-component decomposition: a column-space term based on the Moore--Penrose pseudoinverse, and a null-space correction for perturbations outside the column space of $M$.

</details>


### [296] [Holographic Invariant Storage: Design-Time Safety Contracts via Vector Symbolic Architectures](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.13558)
*Arsenios Scrivens*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: HIS协议利用双极向量符号架构特性，为LLM上下文漂移提供设计时安全契约，包含三个可评估的封闭形式保证：单信号恢复保真度、连续噪声鲁棒性和多信号容量衰减。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决LLM上下文漂移问题，提供设计时可评估的安全契约，使系统工程师能够在部署前预算恢复保真度和码本容量，这是传统计时器或嵌入距离度量无法提供的。

Method: 基于双极向量符号架构构建HIS协议，通过蒙特卡洛模拟验证理论边界，并进行行为实验测试安全重注入效果。

Result: 理论边界得到验证：单信号恢复保真度收敛于1/√2≈0.707，连续噪声鲁棒性为2Φ(1/σ)-1，多信号容量衰减≈√1/(K+1)。行为实验显示在2B规模上安全重注入能提高依从性。

Conclusion: HIS协议为LLM上下文漂移缓解提供了设计时可评估的安全契约，使系统工程师能够在部署前做出可靠决策，为LLM安全应用提供了新工具。

Abstract: We introduce Holographic Invariant Storage (HIS), a protocol that assembles known properties of bipolar Vector Symbolic Architectures into a design-time safety contract for LLM context-drift mitigation. The contract provides three closed-form guarantees evaluable before deployment: single-signal recovery fidelity converging to $1/\sqrt{2} \approx 0.707$ (regardless of noise depth or content), continuous-noise robustness $2Φ(1/σ) - 1$, and multi-signal capacity degradation $\approx\sqrt{1/(K+1)}$. These bounds, validated by Monte Carlo simulation ($n = 1{,}000$), enable a systems engineer to budget recovery fidelity and codebook capacity at design time -- a property no timer or embedding-distance metric provides. A pilot behavioral experiment (four LLMs, 2B--7B, 720 trials) confirms that safety re-injection improves adherence at the 2B scale; full results are in an appendix.

</details>


### [297] [EmDT: Embedding Diffusion Transformer for Tabular Data Generation in Fraud Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.13566)
*En-Ya Kuo,Sebastien Motsch*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 提出EmDT扩散模型用于欺诈检测中的不平衡数据问题，通过UMAP聚类识别欺诈模式，用Transformer去噪网络生成合成欺诈样本，提升下游分类器性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 欺诈检测中数据集不平衡导致分类器偏向多数类（正常交易），对少数类（欺诈交易）识别性能差。现有合成数据生成方法需要改进。

Method: 提出Clustered Embedding Diffusion-Transformer (EmDT)：1) 使用UMAP聚类识别不同欺诈模式；2) 训练带有正弦位置嵌入的Transformer去噪网络，在扩散过程中捕捉特征关系；3) 生成合成欺诈样本后，使用XGBoost等决策树分类器进行分类。

Result: 在信用卡欺诈检测数据集上的实验表明，EmDT相比现有过采样和生成方法显著提升了下游分类性能，同时保持了可比的隐私保护和原始数据中的特征相关性。

Conclusion: EmDT通过结合聚类、扩散模型和Transformer，有效解决了欺诈检测中的不平衡数据问题，生成高质量的合成欺诈样本，提升了分类器性能。

Abstract: Imbalanced datasets pose a difficulty in fraud detection, as classifiers are often biased toward the majority class and perform poorly on rare fraudulent transactions. Synthetic data generation is therefore commonly used to mitigate this problem. In this work, we propose the Clustered Embedding Diffusion-Transformer (EmDT), a diffusion model designed to generate fraudulent samples. Our key innovation is to leverage UMAP clustering to identify distinct fraudulent patterns, and train a Transformer denoising network with sinusoidal positional embeddings to capture feature relationships throughout the diffusion process. Once the synthetic data has been generated, we employ a standard decision-tree-based classifier (e.g., XGBoost) for classification, as this type of model remains better suited to tabular datasets. Experiments on a credit card fraud detection dataset demonstrate that EmDT significantly improves downstream classification performance compared to existing oversampling and generative methods, while maintaining comparable privacy protection and preserving feature correlations present in the original data.

</details>


### [298] [Robust Sequential Tracking via Bounded Information Geometry and Non-Parametric Field Actions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.13613)
*Carlos C. Rodriguez*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 该论文提出了一种解决序列推断架构在处理极端结构化异常值时出现正态化危机的方法，通过非参数场和严格不变的信息分离在统计流形上物理截断空间分布的无限尾部，将参数空间压缩为有限可归一化的概率液滴。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准序列推断架构在面对极端结构化异常值时存在正态化危机，由于在无界参数空间上操作，缺乏适当分离异常值的内在几何结构，导致协方差无限膨胀和均值发散。

Method: 在元先验层面分析推断的抽象序列，通过在无限维空间上极值化作用，使用由预先验锚定的非参数场，利用统计流形上的严格不变Delta（或ν）信息分离物理截断空间分布的无限尾部，将主动参数空间压缩为严格有限、可归一化的概率液滴。

Result: 在三个领域进行实证基准测试：LiDAR机动目标跟踪、高频加密货币订单流和量子态层析成像，证明有界信息几何能够解析地截断异常值，确保鲁棒估计而不依赖于无限尾部分布假设。

Conclusion: 通过有界信息几何方法解决了序列推断架构的正态化危机，为处理极端结构化异常值提供了理论框架和实用解决方案，确保鲁棒估计而不需要无限尾部分布假设。

Abstract: Standard sequential inference architectures are compromised by a normalizability crisis when confronted with extreme, structured outliers. By operating on unbounded parameter spaces, state-of-the-art estimators lack the intrinsic geometry required to appropriately sever anomalies, resulting in unbounded covariance inflation and mean divergence. This paper resolves this structural failure by analyzing the abstraction sequence of inference at the meta-prior level (S_2). We demonstrate that extremizing the action over an infinite-dimensional space requires a non-parametric field anchored by a pre-prior, as a uniform volume element mathematically does not exist. By utilizing strictly invariant Delta (or ν) Information Separations on the statistical manifold, we physically truncate the infinite tails of the spatial distribution. When evaluated as a Radon-Nikodym derivative against the base measure, the active parameter space compresses into a strictly finite, normalizable probability droplet. Empirical benchmarks across three domains--LiDAR maneuvering target tracking, high-frequency cryptocurrency order flow, and quantum state tomography--demonstrate that this bounded information geometry analytically truncates outliers, ensuring robust estimation without relying on infinite-tailed distributional assumptions.

</details>


### [299] [When Should Humans Step In? Optimal Human Dispatching in AI-Assisted Decisions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.13688)
*Lezhi Tan,Naomi Sagan,Lihua Lei,Jose Blanchet*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 提出决策理论框架优化人机协作，通过选择性获取人类判断来修正AI输出，应用于同行评审可达到全人工评审效果但仅需20-30%人工信息。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI生成的评估常存在噪声或系统性偏差，需要高效分配有限的人类资源来修正AI输出，以提升最终决策质量。

Method: 提出决策理论框架，将AI评估视为因子级信号，人类判断作为可选择性获取的成本信息。开发非参数和线性模型下的估计方法，涵盖上下文和非上下文选择规则。

Result: 应用于AI辅助同行评审时，方法显著优于纯LLM预测，性能接近全人工评审但仅需20-30%人工信息。线性模型下的简单规则可大幅降低计算成本而不损害预测性能。

Conclusion: 研究证明了人类干预的价值和原则性分配的高效性，为人机协作提供了理论框架和实用方法。

Abstract: AI systems increasingly assist human decision making by producing preliminary assessments of complex inputs. However, such AI-generated assessments can often be noisy or systematically biased, raising a central question: how should costly human effort be allocated to correct AI outputs where it matters the most for the final decision? We propose a general decision-theoretic framework for human-AI collaboration in which AI assessments are treated as factor-level signals and human judgments as costly information that can be selectively acquired. We consider cases where the optimal selection problem reduces to maximizing a reward associated with each candidate subset of factors, and turn policy design into reward estimation. We develop estimation procedures under both nonparametric and linear models, covering contextual and non-contextual selection rules. In the linear setting, the optimal rule admits a closed-form expression with a clear interpretation in terms of factor importance and residual variance. We apply our framework to AI-assisted peer review. Our approach substantially outperforms LLM-only predictions and achieves performance comparable to full human review while using only 20-30% of the human information. Across different selection rules, we find that simpler rules derived under linear models can significantly reduce computational cost without harming final prediction performance. Our results highlight both the value of human intervention and the efficiency of principled dispatching.

</details>


### [300] [An Interpretable and Stable Framework for Sparse Principal Component Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.13806)
*Ying Hu,Hu Yang*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 提出SP-SPCA方法，通过引入平衡参数自适应调整变量惩罚，在稀疏性和解释方差之间取得更好平衡，在高维噪声数据中表现优异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统稀疏主成分分析（SPCA）对变量施加均匀惩罚，未考虑变量重要性差异，在高噪声或结构复杂场景下性能不稳定。

Method: 提出SP-SPCA方法，在正则化框架中引入单一平衡参数，自适应调整变量惩罚，修改L2惩罚项以灵活控制稀疏性与解释方差的权衡。

Result: 模拟研究显示该方法在识别稀疏载荷模式、过滤噪声变量和保持累积方差方面优于标准稀疏主成分方法，尤其在高压噪声场景。犯罪和金融市场数据的实证应用验证了其实际效用。

Conclusion: 该方法为复杂高维数据的稀疏建模提供了稳健高效的选择，在稳定性、特征选择和可解释性方面具有明显优势。

Abstract: Sparse principal component analysis (SPCA) addresses the poor interpretability and variable redundancy often encountered by principal component analysis (PCA) in high-dimensional data. However, SPCA typically imposes uniform penalties on variables and does not account for differences in variable importance, which may lead to unstable performance in highly noisy or structurally complex settings. We propose SP-SPCA, a method that introduces a single equilibrium parameter into the regularization framework to adaptively adjust variable penalties. This modification of the L2 penalty provides flexible control over the trade-off between sparsity and explained variance while maintaining computational efficiency. Simulation studies show that the proposed method consistently outperforms standard sparse principal component methods in identifying sparse loading patterns, filtering noise variables, and preserving cumulative variance, especially in high-dimensional and noisy settings. Empirical applications to crime and financial market data further demonstrate its practical utility. In real data analyses, the method selects fewer but more relevant variables, thereby reducing model complexity while maintaining explanatory power. Overall, the proposed approach offers a robust and efficient alternative for sparse modeling in complex high-dimensional data, with clear advantages in stability, feature selection, and interpretability

</details>


### [301] [Maximin Robust Bayesian Experimental Design](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.14094)
*Hany Abdulsamad,Sahel Iqbal,Christian A. Naesseth,Takuo Matsubara,Adrien Corenflos*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 提出一种对抗性贝叶斯实验设计方法，通过最大最小博弈处理模型误设，使用Sibson α-互信息作为鲁棒目标，采用PAC-Bayes框架获得有限样本误差控制下的严格下界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 贝叶斯实验设计在模型误设情况下表现脆弱，需要开发鲁棒的设计方法来应对模型不确定性。

Method: 将实验设计问题建模为实验者与对抗性自然之间的最大最小博弈，使用Sibson α-互信息作为鲁棒目标，α-倾斜后验作为鲁棒信念更新，采用PAC-Bayes框架搜索随机设计策略。

Result: 该方法建立了Rényi散度作为条件信息增益的适当度量，通过PAC-Bayes框架获得了对鲁棒期望信息增益的严格高概率下界，能够显式控制有限样本误差。

Conclusion: 提出的对抗性贝叶斯实验设计框架能够有效处理模型误设问题，通过信息论约束和PAC-Bayes方法提供了具有理论保证的鲁棒设计策略。

Abstract: We address the brittleness of Bayesian experimental design under model misspecification by formulating the problem as a max--min game between the experimenter and an adversarial nature subject to information-theoretic constraints. We demonstrate that this approach yields a robust objective governed by Sibson's $α$-mutual information~(MI), which identifies the $α$-tilted posterior as the robust belief update and establishes the Rényi divergence as the appropriate measure of conditional information gain. To mitigate the bias and variance of nested Monte Carlo estimators needed to estimate Sibson's $α$-MI, we adopt a PAC-Bayes framework to search over stochastic design policies, yielding rigorous high-probability lower bounds on the robust expected information gain that explicitly control finite-sample error.

</details>


### [302] [Solving physics-constrained inverse problems with conditional flow matching](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.14135)
*Agnimitra Dasgupta,Ali Fardisi,Mehrnegar Aminy,Brianna Binder,Bryan Shaddy,Assad Oberai*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 提出条件流匹配框架解决物理约束贝叶斯反问题，无需显式计算先验和似然密度，通过神经网络学习概率流ODE的速度场，直接从源分布传输到后验分布。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决传统贝叶斯反问题方法需要显式计算先验和似然密度的限制，处理非线性、高维、不可微前向模型，提供黑箱式解决方案。
